SARGIS OF ABERGA FIRST ASSEMBLY 1. Governor Sargis - * In the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, one God. O my dear (brothers), listen to what happened in the days of Harägaäl, king of Rome, concerning a man called Sargis of Aberga, who (was part) of the king's army. Behold: (the king) made him governor of two provinces, Afrägyä and Tartägyä. As for Sargis, he was a strong and irascible man. 2. The baptism imposed on the Jews. - Thus it is written in the book of his administration. (It is written) how the merciful king ordered all the prefects who (were) under * his dominion, (how) he ordered (them) to

baptize the Jews who had converted to the Lord. When Sargis arrived in the province of Afragya, he ordered all the Jews who (were) under (his) jurisdiction to come to him. When they had arrived, he said to them: "Are you not servants of the king, who listen to his word and fulfill his order?" As for them, they said: "Truly, we are servants of the merciful king." Then, Sargis said to them: "Behold: the king has ordered you to be baptized in the name of the Father, of the Son * and of the Holy Spirit." When they had heard this word, they were seized with great fear; terror gripped them and they could not answer a word. Sargis said to them: "Why are you silent? Will you answer nothing?" Then, one of them, called Jonas, answered and said to him: "We will not do this, for the time has not come (for us) to be baptized in the house of the temple." At that moment, Sargis became angry, leaped up, and struck that man. He said to these men: "You, certainly, are not servants if you do not fulfill the order of your Lord." When they had heard this, fear and terror seized them. Immediately, he ordered them to be baptized by force, against their will. * That is why a great sorrow

came upon them. The one who wrote this book says: "Behold: I am Joseph, one of the Jews who were baptized, and I became a Christian. The Lord knows that a great sorrow reached us and that tortures seized us, because we were baptized against our will."

3. The doctor Jacob. - However, in the multitude of his mercy, the Lord, merciful and lenient, who desires salvation and peace for his servants, sent to us an intelligent man, a preceptor of the Law and of the rule, called Jacob, who was from the land of the East, from the city called Accä. As for him, he had read many writings of the prophets. He came to our country to live (there) * sold his property, and, when he had heard the news that Sargis was having the Jews baptized, he feared, was worried, changed his clothes, and became like a Christian. Then, the Lord sent a man to buy goods from him; it could have been three garments. When it was evening, at the moment when the sun sets, this buyer said to him: "Go away, return to your house, and tomorrow come so that I may pay you for your goods." Then, Jacob rose (and took leave) of him. He went down the steps; his feet made a false step. He said in the Hebrew language:

ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ እንተ ፡ ሰዋስው ፡ ወድኅፀ'፣ እገሪሁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ዕብራይስጥ" ፡ አዶናይ ፣ አዶናይ ፣ ተሣሀለኒ" = ወሰሚዖ ፡ ባዕለ" -፣ ቤት ፣ ”አአመረ" ፣ ከመዝ ፤ አይሁዳዊ ፣ ውእቱ ፡= ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ሖረ፣ ኀበ ፣ ሰርጊስ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ወነገሮ" ፡ ዜናሁ ፣ ለውእቱ ፣ አይሁዳዊ ፡ ዘይትመሰል ፡ ከ መ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ያምጽእዎ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወዕበ' ፡ መጽአ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ተ ጠመቅ ፡ አበየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኢበጽሐ"፣ ጊዜሁ ፣ ለተጠምቆ ፡ በቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡=፡ ወ አዘዘ ፡ ይደይዎ ፡ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ፻ዕለተ ፡፡ ፡፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አውጽ እዎ ፡ እምቤተ" ፣ ሞቅሕ ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፣ ተጠመቅ ። ፡= አበየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ድል ው ፡ አነ ፡ ለተሰቅሎ ፡ ወለተቀትሎ ፡ ግሙራ ፤ ኢይጠመቅ" ፡ አንሰ ፡፡ ወበጊዜሃ ፡ አ
"Adonay, Adonay, show yourself lenient toward me." Having heard (these words), the master of the house * knew that this one was a Jew. Immediately, he went to the governor Sargis and explained to him the story of this Jew, who had become like the Christians. Then, (Sargis) ordered him to be brought to him; when (Jacob) had come, he said to him: "Be baptized." But (Jacob) did not want to and said: "The time has not come to be baptized in the house of the temple." (Sargis) ordered him to be thrown into prison, and he remained there for a hundred days. Then, they brought him out of prison and said to him: "Be baptized." He refused and said: "Behold: I am ready to be crucified and to be killed. Never, as for me, will I be baptized." Immediately, they seized him and * they baptized him against his will.

4. Vision of Jacob. - Behold: a thought came into him which said: "Perhaps because of this, faith in justice will come to me." Then, he began to search and to ask the Lord (for) long days; he raised his eyes toward him and said: "O my Lord and my God, if this law is right, reveal (it) to me and do not hide (it) from me." That night, he saw in a dream a man, who was dressed in purple, (who) came toward

ሌሊት ፡ ርእየ ፡ በሕልም ፣ ብእሴ ፣ ዘይለብስ ፤ ሜላተ' ፣ መጽአ ፣ ኅቤሁ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስከ ፡ ማእዜኑ ፡ ተአቢ ፡ ብሂለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወልደ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፤ ውእቱ ፡= ኢትሌቡኑ ፡፤ ዘነበበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በአፈ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ወል ድየ ፣ አንተ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ዮም ፡ ወለድኩከ" ፡።፡ ወእምውእቱ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አኀዘ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ይኅሥሥ" ፣ አንብቦ ፡ መጻሕፍት" ፡ ብሉይ ፡ ወሐዲስ ። ወይለቡ" ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ዘውስቴቶ ሙ ፣ ወይስአል፤ ፤ አእምሮ ፡ ምጽአቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፣ አግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያ ው = ወእምዝ ፣ ጠየቆ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ዘመጽአ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ አውግስ
him and said to him: "Until when will you refuse to say that Christ is the Son of the living Lord? Will you not understand what the Lord has stated, through the mouth of the prophet David who says: * You are my Son. I myself have begotten you today." From that day on, Jacob began to search for the reading of the Scriptures of the Old and New (Testament), to understand the words that (were found) in them, and to inquire to know the coming of Christ, the Son of the living Lord. Then, he realized that he was the Christ, the One who had come in the days of Augustus Caesar, (and) who was born in Bethlehem.

5. Conversion of Jacob. - After a few days, we met Jacob. Then, he said to us: "Concerning this, O brothers, I see that you are sorrowful and that you are sad." We said to him: "Yes, truly, we are sorrowful and we are sad, because * we have been baptized." || said to us: "O brothers, do not be sorrowful and do not be sad because of this, but rather, rejoice and exult. Come, let us go to a place, so that I may reveal to you what you need to know: (namely) that Our Lord Jesus Christ has come into the world, so that you do not despise him in your heart."


[p. 560] {#po.3.p560}
ክሙ ፡። ተሰጥዎ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡፣ ብየ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ኅ ቡአ' ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይሬእዮ" ፡= ወእምዝ ፡ ሖርነ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወተጋባእነ ፡ ህየ"፣ወ ዐጸውነ ፡ ኀዋኅወ ።
One of us, called Isaac, answered him and said to him: "I have a hidden dwelling. No one will see it." Then, we went with him; we gathered there and we closed the doors.

6. The coming of Christ. - Jacob began to give us an exposition. He said: "Know, O my brothers * and my fellow citizens, that the Law and the prophets began by announcing the coming of Christ, Son of the living Lord. Behold: the Sun of Justice has come, concerning whom the Law and the prophets have prophesied, (saying) that he will be of the stock of Jesse and of Judah, the One who will reveal faith and righteousness, and will give life to every being who will have turned toward him. Indeed, the prophets have prophesied, by the Holy Spirit, concerning his coming, his Incarnation, his birth, all the miracles that he did, his sufferings, his crucifixion, his death, his resurrection from the dead, his ascent into the heavens, * from where he had descended, his second coming, in great glory, and, furthermore, concerning the coming of the false Messiah. (They have prophesied) how (the latter) will remain on the earth seven years and three months, and how also he will perish, when the true Christ comes, at the end of the world, and (when he) will reward each one according to his works.


[p. 561] {#po.3.p561}
በከመ ፡ይቤ ፡ ዳንኤል ፤ነቢይ ። ወርኢኩ ፡ በራእየ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወናሁ ፡ መጽአ ፡ በ ደመና ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ከመ ፣ ወልደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ወበጽሐ' ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሉየ ፡ መዋዕ ል ፡ ወተውሀበ ፦፡ ሎቱ ፡ ምኩናን ፡ ወክብር ፡ ወመንግሥት ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ወ በሓውርት" ፡ ወነገድ ፤ ”ወይትቀነዩ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ወምኩናኑሂ ፡ምዙናን ፡ ዘለዓለመ ፡ ዓለ ም ፡ ዘኢየኅልቅ" ፡ ወመንግሥት ፡ ዘኢይማስን ።፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ተነበዩ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወ አለበዉነ" ፣ጊዜ ፣ ምጽአቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢንማስን ፣ ወኢንስሐት" ፡ ፍኖቶ ፡ ወኢንትወከ ፍ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ክርስቶስ" ፡ ዘበ ፣ አማን ፣ ዘመጽአ ፣ ለመድኀኒተ ፡ እለ ፡ የ አምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡=፡ ኦአኅኀውየ' ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክነ" ፡ በአፈ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በመዝሙር ‹፥ ዘወ፪ ፡፡ አመ ፡ ሰማዕክሙ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ኢታጽንዑ ፡ልበክሙ ።፡ ወኢታምር ርዎ" ፡ ከመ ፡ አበዊክሙ ፡ በገዳም" ፡ ኦአኅው"' ፡ አይሁድሰ ፡ በከመ ፡ ታአምሩ” ፡ ያ ስተበቀነዑ” ፡ መዐልተ ፣ ወሌሊተ ፡ ይርአዩ ፡ ምጽአቶ” ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ ዘይ ሴፎ ፡ ዙሉ” ፡ ፍጥረተ” ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው" ፡=፡
As the prophet Daniel (has) said: I saw in a vision of the night; behold: on a cloud of heaven came one like a son of man. He arrived toward the Ancient of Days. They gave him dominion, glory, and the kingdom. All peoples, nations, and tribes * served him. As for his dominion, it is a dominion for ages of ages, which will not pass away, and (his) kingdom is (a kingdom) which will not perish. That is why the prophets have prophesied and have taught us the time of the coming (of Christ) so that we do not perish, that we do not wander (off) his path, and that we do not receive another (Christ) than the true Christ, who has come for the salvation of those who will believe in him. O my brothers, the Lord, our God, has said, through the mouth of the prophet David, in Psalm XCIV: When you have heard the voice (of the Lord), do not harden your heart. Do not exasperate him, like your fathers in the desert. O (my) brothers, the Jews, as you know, * pray, day and night, in order to see the coming of the true Christ, whom every human creature hopes for."

7. The unbelief of the Jews. - However, as for us, we have been impious and we have sinned, (for) we have thought, in our heart, that He who was born of Mary, of the holy Virgin, was not the Christ. Never

have we wanted to hear his mention, nor the Scriptures of the prophets, who have prophesied concerning him. Although we read the Scriptures of the prophets, we want to interpret (them) as our soul desires. That is why our heart has hardened and has thickened. As the prophet Isaiah (has) said: Why have you made us wander, O Lord, (off) your path and have hardened our heart, * so that we do not fear you? That is why the Lord has ordered us, in the Holy Scriptures, to pray and to raise our eyes toward him, so that we do not wander (off) his path. Behold: as for us, we have abandoned all this, because of the regulation of the Sabbath; we have been rebellious and we have said: "Christ is slow to come." We have rejected the word of the prophets, who have prophesied concerning him. We have reached the height of ignominy, of reproach, of abjection, and of rejection among all peoples. We have begun to eat, to the point of bursting, and to drink, to the point of getting drunk. We have fornicated. We have committed sin. We have received bribes. We have loved the accumulation of riches. We have judged, to capture favors. We have done all

(kinds) of * evil works. We have transgressed all the commandments of the Lord. As for what is in righteousness, it is fitting that we seek (it) with all our heart. We have neglected and we have not meditated on the coming of Our Lord Jesus Christ. We have not believed in him. We have said: "Why is he slow to come?" Indeed, the prophet who desired that (Christ) would come, has said: Behold: he will come and he will not delay. 8. Worries of Jacob. - Jacob said such (words) to us: "The Lord knows that, when I had been spoken to concerning Our Lord Jesus Christ, that I had been baptized, against my will, and that I had been made a Christian, from that day on, O (my) brothers, I have not been tranquil, (neither) day, nor night; it has not been pleasant for me to eat, nor to drink; * but, on the contrary, I have interceded continually before the Lord, through fasting, through prayer, through tears, by reading the Scriptures of the Law and of the prophets. I have continued to bring the Scriptures to Rome, to go to the Church and to the oratories (τόπος), and to ask the Christians to interpret the Scriptures (for me). Then, I meditated (on them) in my heart and I said to my soul: "O my soul, will sin

therefore come upon me, because I have been baptized?" I found, in what I was reading, a prophecy of Moses. Jacob, son of Isaac, son of Abraham, said, by the Holy Spirit, a prophecy concerning Christ. He said in the Law: O Judah, * your brothers will glorify you. Your hands will be on the back of your enemies. The sons of your father will bow down before you. Judah is a lion's cub. Rise from your lair, my son. You will lie down, you will sleep like a lion and like a lion's cub. No one will wake you. The dominion will not disappear from Judah, (nor) the principality from his members, until He who is expected for (Israel) comes and is the hope of the peoples. As for us, O (my) brothers, behold: we see that Christ, the hope of the peoples, has come.

paths of wisdom and he has given it to Jacob, his servant, and to Israel, his beloved. Then, he appeared on the earth and he became like a man. Again, Isaiah said: Behold: a virgin will conceive and will give birth to a son. He will be called Emmanuel, (which) (means) in its interpretation: The Lord is with us. Furthermore, the prophet Isaiah said: I went toward the prophetess woman. She conceived and gave birth to a son. * The Lord said to me: Call him: Quickly activate the pursuit of the plunder. 9. The work of Christ. - Yes, truly, (O my) brothers, behold: Christ has pursued death, has redeemed the prey from the hand of the devil, in Sheol, and has made the worship of idols cease. As for us, Jews, who have not believed in him, he has driven us out, has taken us captive (out) of our regions and (of) our dwelling, and has scattered us among all peoples. This is to demonstrate (the truth) of the word of our father, the prophet Jacob, who says to his son Judah, in the Law: Your hands will be on the back of your enemies. The sons of your father will bow down before you. O (my) brothers, see that what Moses said concerning Jacob, by this word, is exact. Know, O (my) dear (brothers), * that Adam, the father of us all, when he had sinned, the devil subjected him with his children in an evil slavery. As (the


[p. 566] {#po.3.p566}
ዚአብሔር ፡ በአፈ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ ነቢይ ፡ እስመ ፡ አማልክተ' ፡፣ አሕዛብ ፡ አጋንንት" ፣ ኦሪትሰ ፣ ወነቢያት ፡ ኢይክህሉ" ፡ አድኅኖቶ ፣ ለውሉደ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምእደ ፡ ሰ ይጣን" = እስመ ፡ ብዙኃን" ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ለባውያን" ፡ ወመምሀራነ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ስሕቱ' ፡፡ ወ ነቢያትሰ ፡ አቅደሙ ፡ ዜንዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ መድኀኒተ" ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ትዝ
Lord has) said, through the mouth of the prophet David: Indeed, the gods of the peoples are demons. As for the Law and the prophets, they could not save the sons of man from the hand of Satan, for many kings, scholars, and doctors of the Law have wandered. As for the prophets, they announced in advance that Christ will become the salvation of the whole race of men.

10. What Christ is. - When the Word of the Lord came, he became man from the Virgin Mary, of the lineage of David. He was pleased to become man. He became like men and he appeared in * their aspect. Indeed, it is not possible for man to see the Son of the Lord, except only in the flesh. When he had become man, his hands were on the back of his enemies as our father, the prophet Jacob, (has) said. As for Christ, by the flesh, he is man: but, by the Word, he is the creator God. In his Incarnation, he appeared weak to them, but, when they heard the Word of the divinity, they fled. When he stretched out his hands on the cross, his enemies were covered with shame and lost hope. That is why it is fitting that every knee bow and incline before Christ, for he has become for us like a brother, by the flesh

that he took from * Mary. By his divinity, he conquered his enemies, so that no one would be proud and say: "I will not bow down before a flesh, which is, like me, created." As for us, we bow down, we incline before (the Word) and we glorify the Word of the Lord, who took flesh from us. As the prophet Daniel (has) predicted: I saw on a cloud of heaven one like a son of man. He arrived toward the Ancient of Days. They gave him dominion, glory, and the kingdom. All peoples, nations, and tribes served him. The prophet Isaiah said: Indeed, A child is born to us; a son is given to us. The primacy will be on his shoulder. || will be called the Angel of the great counsel, for he will bring * peace for the princes. As for his own life, he will give (it) before him. There will be no end before him. His peace will be on the throne of David. His kingdom will be solid. || will approve justice and righteousness from now and forever. 11. Coming of Christ. - Have you seen, O (my) brothers, that this word demonstrates and proves that Christ, the Word of the Lord, who is existing with the Father, has come, has taught us the mystery of his divinity, as man, and has given us the New Testament, that is to say, the Holy Gospel? Have you heard what Moses said: I will raise up for them a prophet from among their brothers who (will be) like you. I will put my word in his mouth. This (prophet) is Christ who brought the Holy Gospel. Furthermore, the prophet * Jeremiah said, concerning the Testament of Christ, that there will come, after the law of Moses, another Testament which will be greater than it. 1] said: Behold: days are coming, says the Lord. the house of Israel and for the house of Judah a new Testament, not (a Testament) like the Old Testament that I established for their fathers when I took them by the hand and made them come out of the land of Egypt, for, certainly, they did not remain in (the observance of) my covenant. As for me, I neglected them, says the Lord. Such is the Testament that I will establish for the house of Israel, after those * days, says the Lord. I will put my Law in their heart. I will write it in their intelligence. I will therefore make them (my people) and I will become their God. They themselves will become my people. No one will teach anymore, who, his fellow citizen, who, his brother (nor) will say to them: "Know the Lord", for all will know me, small and great, (and will know) that I will forgive their sin and that I will no longer remember their fault.

Furthermore, He said: This word will not end, (but will last) until the third and fourth generation. * I will take vengeance. However, I will not claim (the punishment) for the sin of the son in place (of that) of his father, but (I will act) as if it had not been said: "The ancestors ate green fruit; as for the children, their teeth were set on edge." But, on the contrary, he who eats green fruit will be the one whose teeth will be set on edge. Behold then: the Law, indeed, demonstrated the coming of Our Lord Jesus Christ, in the flesh, for the race of men cannot see the Word of the Lord who is invisible, except, however, in the flesh that He took from us. not by deputation, (but) it is in person that He will come to save them. Again, * He said concerning Christ: This is my son whom I love and in whom I am well pleased; I will place my spirit upon him. He will bring forth justice for the peoples. He will not cry out; he will not vociferate; his voice will not be heard outside; he will not break the bruised reed; he will not quench the smoking wick. On the contrary, by justice he will restore right; he will shine; he will not disappear, until justice returns to the earth. The peoples will place (their) trust in his own name. 13. Christ the Savior. - Have you noticed, O (my) brothers, as the Lord, the master of all, said, that by the name of Jesus Christ every creature will live? This (Jesus) is the Christ who * was born of the Virgin Mary. Furthermore, He said: Behold: the Old (Testament) has passed; the New is revealed. I will make you hear it. What is the Old (Testament)? Is it not the Law? And what is the New, if not only the Holy Gospel? The prophet David said: Glorify the Lord (with) a new praise. Somewhere, he said: Glorify Him from the ends of the earth, from the depth of the sea and in all the islands. The sea is the world. Will it be

* See then, O (my) brothers, what (Scripture) said; (it said) that it is not by the Law of Moses that human creatures will be saved, but only that it is by the New Testament that they will be saved and will live. It is the book of Wisdom, (in) which the Lord commanded to listen to the word of the Law which will remain forever. Every being who believes in it will live. But, for him who will not have believed in it, let him die truly in flesh and (in) spirit. We must, O (my) brothers, accept this book and become strong through it, so that we may live and not perish. Indeed, (this book) said: Turn, O Jacob, (toward the Lord) and cover yourself in His light, for it is by His light that you will live. But now, (O my brothers), I (am going to) explain to you that the New Testament of Christ is light, justice * and eternal life. Indeed, the prophets prophesied that it is of the Holy Spirit. As for him who will not have believed in Him, he will go to perdition

We must not, therefore, (O my) brothers, live by the Law of the Jews, after Christ, the king of the whole world, has come. Indeed, the prophet Jeremiah said: Behold: days will come, said the Lord: I will establish for David a light of justice, in this place. We know that David is the father of Christ by the flesh. Behold: (Christ) will reign and will do righteousness and justice, on the earth. In * the days (of Christ), Judah and Israel will not be separated, but they will remain preserved. (Christ) will be called Emmanuel. 14. Obedience to Christ. - You have heard the justice that will come for him who will have believed in Christ. Do not separate yourselves, O (my) brothers, and do not turn away from faith (in) Our Lord Jesus Christ, our king and our God." Those who were with Jacob answered and said: "Why did Moses say: Keep this Law, so that one may live by it? As for you, Jacob, you said: "We must not live by the Law of the Jews, nor keep the Sabbath." As for us, we can keep

በ' ፡ ሰንበተ" ፡ ወኢነ”"አምን ፣ በክርስቶስ ፡፡ አውስአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሰበ" ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ሙሴ ፡ ይዕቀቡ' ፡ ሕገ ፡ ዘወሀቦሙ!" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ፅ ቀቡ ፡ኪያሁ ።፡ ወአመ ፡ ቦእክሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ እንተ ፡ ይሁበክሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር፣ አምላክክሙ ፡ ኢትትመሀሩ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ርኩሶሙ" ፡ ለውእቶሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እ ስመ ፡ ስሕቱ ፡ በእኩይ' ፡ ምግባር ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ መነኖሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ።፡ ወአንተ ሰ ፡ እስራኤል!" ፡ ኢትግበር ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘይሁበከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምውስተ ፡ አኀዊሆሙ ፡ ዘከማየ ፣ ወያነሥእ"፣ ለከ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ አምላክከ ፣ ሎ"”ቱ ፡ ስምዕ ዎ"፣ወተአዘዙ
the Sabbath and we do not believe * in Christ." Jacob answered and said to them: "When Moses commanded to keep the Law, which the Lord had given (to the Israelites), he said to the people: Keep it and, when you have entered the land that the Lord, your God, will give you, do not learn to act according to the impurity of the peoples. Indeed, they have wandered in evil actions. That is why the Lord rejected them. As for you, Israel, do not act thus. The prophet whom the Lord will give you will be from among (your) brothers, he who (will be) like me. The Lord, your God, will raise (him) up for you. * Listen to him and obey (him).
ወይእዜሰ ፡ አኀው ፡ ናሁ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ዝውእቱ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወተሰብአ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ውላጤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አራቂ ፡ ማእከለ" ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡=፡ ወእመሰ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ ይትቀነዩ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ ወይስምዑ” ፡ ቃሎ ።፡ ወናሁ ፡መጽአ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወዘሰ ፡ ኢአምነ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወኢሰምዐ” ፡ ቃሎ ፕ፡ ይትቃረኖ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ በደኃሪ" ፡ ፅለት ፡ እስመ ፡ ዐለወ ፣ ትእዛዞ” ፡፡ ይቤ ፡ ምልክያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወይሠርቅ ፡ ፀሓየ “ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ ለአለ ፡ ይፈርሁ ፡ ስምየ ፡
But now, (O my) brothers, behold: Christ, that is to say the Word of the Lord, has come. He became man, without change. He became the reconciler between men and the Lord. That is why Moses commanded that one be subject to this prophet and that one listen to his word. Behold: (this prophet) has come; it is Christ. As for him who will not have believed in Him and who will not have listened to His word, Moses will struggle against him on the last day, for he has transgressed His commandment. The prophet Malachi said: The sun of justice will rise for those who

fear my name. Life will be under the wings (of Christ). David said: The day is yours and the night is yours. Behold: the Lord has established * the light of the moon and the stars, (as) a guide in the night, so that men may go (their) way on the earth. For those who travel on the sea, they also go to the splendor (of this light). When the morning star, that is to say the sun, has risen, the light of the day makes the light of the moon, the stars, and the luminaries cease, which shine in the night and do not shine with the light of the sun, in the day, due to the intensity of the light (of the sun). Indeed, it is not that I say that the light of the moon and the stars is not good. Far from it (such a thought) for Him who created them. But rather, He created them, giving them the power to shine in the darkness of the night. Such are, therefore, the Law and the prophets. They remained, in their time, * in the image of the moon, the stars, and the luminaries, (and) shone for (men), because of the power of the devil who had subjected men, in those

ሎቱ ፡፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘያነቅህ ፡ ልቦ' ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ፡ አምብዝኀ" ፡ ጽል መት ፡ዘኮነ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ ወፈድፋደ" ፡ ተወሰኩ ፡ አበሳ'፣ ወኀጢአት ፡=፡" ወቦ ፡እምኔ ሆሙ ፡ አብዳን ፡ ዘይቤሉ ፡ እምአመ ፡ ሰማዕክሙ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ በዝኀ ፡ ላዕሌነ"፣ እኪት = ወእምዝ ፣ ተመይጡ ፡ ደቂቀ' ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ ጣዖት" ፡ ወተለዉ" ፡ ርሹሶሙ ፡፡ ወአመ ፡ ”መጽአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ፀሓየ" ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለ ም ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ፈውሶ ፡ ሕማመነ ' ፡ አሥረቀ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ሕጉ ፡ ለሐዲስ ፡ ሥርዐት” ፡ ወንሕነሰ ፡ ኢገነይነ ፡ ወኢተለውነ” ፡ ለዘ ፡ ከሠተ” ፡ ለነ ፡ መንክራተ ።፡
days. Indeed, he had power over the souls of men, over the just and the sinners, at the same time, (who) obeyed him. None (of them) awakened his heart toward the Lord, his creator, because of the multitude of darkness that had come upon them. Crime and sin increased enormously. There were among them fools who said: "Every time you heard the word of the prophets, the evil was considerable upon us." Then, the children of Israel, the people of the Lord, turned toward the worship of idols and adhered to their defilement. (But), when * Our Lord Jesus Christ, the Son of the Lord, that is to say the sun of justice, the light of the whole world, (He) who can heal our sickness, came, He made the strong light of the Law of the New Testament rise upon us. As for us, we did not bow (before Him) and
በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ” ፡ ነቢይ ።፡ ጽንዑ ፡ እደው ፡ ፅቡሳት”" ፡ ወእገር ፡ ሐንካሳ ት" ናዝዝዎን ፡ ለአልባቢክሙ ፡ ዕንቡዛን ፡ ወኢትፍርሁ ” ፡፡ ናሁ ፣ አምላክነ ፡ ያገብ እ ፡ ፍትሐ ፡ ወይትቤቀል”፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ወይመጽእ ፡ ወያድኅነነ” ፡፡ ውእተ ፡ አሚረ ፡ የሐ ይዉ ፡ አፅይንተ ፡ ዕውራን ፡ ወይሰምዑ ፡ ጽሙማን ፡፡ ውአተ ፣ አሚረ ፡ ይረውጹ ፡
we did not follow Him who had revealed wonders to us. 15. Christ the liberator. - As the prophet Isaiah said: Be strong, O weak hands and lame feet! Console your hearts, O faint-hearted, and do not fear! Behold: our God will restore right; He Himself will punish; He will come and He will save us. That day, the eyes of the blind will be healed and the deaf will hear. That day, the lame will run like the deer and the tongue of the mute will be

ከመ ፡ሀየል፤' ፡ ሐንካሳን" ፡ ወይረትዕ" ፡ ልሳነ" ፣ በሃም" ።፡ ወለእለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ”ውስ ተ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ ወጽላሎተ ፡ ሞት ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ሠረቀ ፡ ሎሙ ። ወእምይእዜሰ ፣ ኦአኀ ው ፡ ኢንፈቅድ ፡ እንከ ፣ ብርሃነ" ፡ ወርኅ" ፣ ወከዋክብት ፡ ወመኃትው" ፡ አለ ፡ ይበር ሁ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ለአለ ፣ ያመልኩ ፡ ጣዖተ" ፡፡ ለነሰ ፡ የአክለነ” ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ፀሓየ" ፡ ጽ ድቅ ፡ዘበ ፡ አማን” ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተከሥተ” ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ ያበርሁ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ በአ ምሳለ” ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ወከዋክብት ፡ በሌሊት ፡፡ ሌሊትሰ ፡ ይተረጐም” ፣ ጽልመተ” ፡ ምግ ባረ ፡ ኀጢአት ።
restored (to health). For those who dwell * in darkness and the shadow of death, the light has risen for them. But, from now on, O (my) brothers, we no longer need the light of the moon, the stars, and the luminaries, which shine in the night; (that is good) for those who worship idols. But, for us, Christ, the true sun of justice, suffices. Behold: the Law was revealed and the prophets enlightened men, in the image of the moon and the stars, in the night. As for the night, it signifies the darkness of the work of sin.
ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይመጽእ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ተግሣጽ ፡ እምኅበ ፣ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ በአፈ ፡ ነቢያቲሁ ፡ በእንተ ፣ እከየ ፡ ምግባሮሙ ፣ ወስሕተቶሙ ፡ ወክሕደቶ ሙ ፡ ወ”ርኩፅሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አማልክት" ፡ ነኪር ፡፡ ወባሕቱሰ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ፀሓይ” ፣ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘነበበ ፡ በኦሪት ፣ ወነቢያት ፡ው እቱኬ ፤ ዘገሠጾሙ ፡ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ወአብዝኀ” ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ እምኅበ” ፡ እ ግዚአብሔር ፡፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይበቀዖሙ”ኖ ፣ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤፡ ዳጅእሙ” ፣ ዘተወሰ
(The sinners) remained (in their sins), when the admonition from the Lord came upon them, (announced) by the mouth of His prophets, concerning the malice of their work, their straying, their infidelity, and * their defilement with foreign gods. However, the voice of the Lord, of the Holy Spirit, who spoke in the Law and the prophets, was not like the light of the sun. This (voice), which admonished them, taught them, and multiplied upon them the teachings from the Lord, served them in nothing, except, however, to increase the straying and the infidelity. They

remained in darkness and sin, as (in) the darkness of the night. Behold: their eyes were darkened and their heart became thickened, so that they would not see the light of the Lord, the Most High - may His name be blessed! They became like blind men, who cannot look at the moon, the stars * and the luminaries. 16. Christ the light. - When the light of the sun of justice rose, that is to say Our Lord Jesus Christ, He made His splendor shine upon the whole world. But, from now on, it is not just that we sleep in the darkness of the night, as we remained formerly in the light of the Law and the prophets, which does not resemble the light of the sun. Behold: formerly, our eyes were weighed down with sleep. We were impious and we were unfaithful. Our eyes were darkened. Sin covered us, as if it were a healthy and warm (garment) while the light of the doctrine of the Law and the prophets was with us. As for the Israelites, their trust was to keep the
ወዕበ” ፡ ሠረቀ ፡ ኮከብ ፣ ዘ”ይመርሕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ዘውእቱ ፣ ዮሐን
Sabbath which has no utility. When the star that * leads to the good light rose, that is to say John, son of Zechariah, (the latter) announced the coming of the sun of justice upon

those who dwell in darkness and the shadow of death, (acting) like the prophet Elijah. The prophet Elijah, indeed, will not come as (in) his first coming. Indeed, the first coming of Our Lord Jesus Christ was in humility, in gentleness, in the likeness of men, so that He might heal our sicknesses and our pains. As for the second (coming), it will not be thus. Indeed, the prophet Isaiah said concerning the preaching of John, son of Zechariah, (and) the coming of Christ: It is the voice of the crier in the desert. Make straight the path of the Lord and level His road. * Let every abyss be filled; let every hill and (every) mountain be lowered; let the rough path become a straight road; let the rough path be leveled. Every (being, endowed) with a soul, will see the salvation of the Lord, for the mouth of the Lord has spoken thus.
ወዳዊትኒ” ፡ ይቤ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዙልክሙ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ምድር ፡ አድኅኖቶ ፡ ለአምላክነ ፡=፡ ወካዕበኖ፣ ይቤ ፡ ወይወርድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጠል ፡ውስተ ፡ ፀምር ፡፡ ወከመ ፡ ነጠብጣብ” ፡ ዘያ ንጠበጥብ” ፡ዲበ ፡ ምድር ።፡ ወክዕበ” ፡ ይቤ ፡ እምቅድመ” ፡ ፀሐይ” ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ስሙ
David said: See, all you ends of the earth, the salvation of our God. Again, he said: He will descend, like dew upon the wool and like drops that drip upon the earth. Again, he said: Before the sun, His name existed. Before the moon, He was for generations of generations.


[p. 580] {#po.3.p580}
እምቅድመ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ለትውልደ ፡ ትውልድ ።፡ ወካዕበ' ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወይትባረኩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዙ ሎሙ ፡ አሕዛበ ፡ ምድር ፡= ወአስተብጽእዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡፤ አሕዛብ ፡=፡ ይትባረክ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ አምላከ" ፡ እስራኤል ፡፡ ዘገብረ ፡ መንክረ" ፡ ባሕቲቱ! ፡=፡ ወይትባረክ ፡ ስመ፤' ፡ ስብሐቲሁ" ፡፡ ለዓለም « ወለዓለም ፡=፡
Again, he said: All the peoples of the earth will be blessed in Him and all the peoples will declare Him blessed. * May the Lord, the God of Israel, who alone has done wonders, be blessed! May His glorious name be blessed forever and ever.
አእምሩ ፡ ኦአኀው ፡ ከመ ፣ ክርስቶስ' ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱኬ ፡ አምላ ከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ዘይብልዎ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡=፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ዳዊ ት" ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ በስመ" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡። እስመ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወልደ ፣ ዳዊ ት እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ አስተርአየ ፡ ለነ ።= ወእመሰ ፡ ትብሉ ፣ ዘንተ፣ቃለ፣ በእንተ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወልደ ፣ ዳዊት =፡ እብለክሙ ፡፣ ዝሰ ፡ ሐሰት ፡ ነገረ ፡ ከንቱ ፡ ውእ ቱ ፡=፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፤ ከመ ፡ እምሥርወ" ፡ አሴይ ፡ ውእቱ ።፡፡ ወዘ ይወፅእ'"' ፡ እምኔሁ ፣ ይነግሥ ፡ ለዓለም ፡፡ ወይትዌከሉ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ አሕዛብ =፡ ወሰሎሞን ሰ ፡ ኢነግሠ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወኢተወከሉ ፡ አሕዛብ” ፡ ቦቱ
17. Christ God. - Know, O (my) brothers, that Christ, the Son of the Lord, is therefore the God of Israel, of whom it was said: He is blessed for ages of ages. As David said: Blessed is He who will come in the name of the Lord. Indeed, Christ is son of David. The Lord, Himself, appeared to us. But, if you say this word concerning Solomon, son of David, I will say to you: "As for this lie, it is a vain word." For the prophet Isaiah said that (Christ) was of the stock of Jesse, (that) He who * would come out of it would reign forever and (that) the peoples would place (their) trust in Him. Now, Solomon did not reign over the whole world and the peoples did not place (their) trust in him.
ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ወይሰግዱ ፡ ለከ ፣ ወይጹልዩ”" ፣ ኀቤከ ፣ አስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ም ስሌከ ። ወአልቦ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእንበሌከ" ፣ ባፅደ” ፡ አንተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአአመርናከ” ፡ አምላከ ፣ ማሕየዌ'"' ፡ አምላከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ መድኅነ” ፡፡ ወክዕበ” ፣
Furthermore, (Isaiah) said: They will prostrate themselves before you and they will pray to you, for the Lord is with you. There is no other Lord than you. You are the Lord.

ይቤ ፡ ወይትረፀዐይ ፡ ተኩላ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አባግዕ ፡ ወያዐርፍ ፡ ነምር ፣፤ ምስለ ፤፡ አጣሊ ፡ወ ይትረዐይ ፤ ላህም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዐንበሳ' ፡ ወየሐስሎሙ" ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ንስቲት ፡=፡ ወይትረዐዩ ፡ አልህምት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ድብ ፡ ኅቡረ ፣ ወይትሐፀኑ ፤ እጐሊሆሙ ፡ ኅቡረ = ወይቀምሕ፣ ዐን"በሳ" ፣ ሐሠረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ላሀም ፡=፡ ወአሞጽኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ይእተ ፡ አሚረ ፡ አሐንጽ ፡ ለቤተ ፣ ዳዊት'፣እንተ፣ ወድቀት ፡=፡ ወኣነሥአ" ፣ ወእነድቃ ፣ ከመ ፣ መዋፅሊሆሙ" ፣ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሥዋ' ፡ እ ለ ፡ ተርፉ፣ሰብእ ። ወኩሉ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እለ ፡ ዲቤሆሙ ፡ ተሰምየ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አግዚአብሔ ር ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ዘንተ ፡=፡ ናሁ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ።፡፡ ወየኅብር" ፡ ቀሥም"፣ምስለ ፡ ማአረር ፡ ወይትረከብ ፡ ዘርእ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሠርፅ ፡ ወያንጸፈጽፍ ፡ መዓር ፡ እምአድባር ፡ ወያሕመለምል”" ፡ አውግር ፡ ወእመይጥ ፡ ፄዋ ፡ ሕዝብየ ፡ እስራኤል ፡፡
We recognize you (as) the life-giving God, the God of Israel, the Savior. Again, he said: The wolf will graze with the sheep; the leopard will rest with the goats; the ox will graze with the lion; a little child will lead them by the halter; the oxen will graze jointly with the bears, and their young will be raised together; the lion will graze * on straw, like the ox. 18. Christ King. - The prophet Amos said: That day, I will (re)build the house of David which has fallen. I will (re)raise it and I will (re)construct it as formerly so that the men who remain and all the peoples, upon whom (my name) has been invoked, may seek it, said the Lord who will do this. Behold: days will come, said the Lord: the harvest will go hand in hand with the reaping, the sowing will meet with the germination, honey will drip from the mountains, the hills will turn green. I will bring back the captives of my people Israel.
ወሰሎሞንሰ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ ኢኮነ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ወኢመድኅነ" ፡ ወኢቤዘወ ፡ ቂዋ ዌ ፡ ዘፄወወ”፣ዲያብሎስ ፡ በአስሕቶ ፡፡ ወአሙንቱሰ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ "እንዘ ፡ ይትቀነዩ ፡ ታሕ ተ፣ሥልጣኑ” ወባሕቱ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ አበሰ ፡ በርስእናሁ"' ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አግዚአብሔር ፡
Indeed, Solomon, son of David, did not become God, nor Savior. He did not redeem the captives that the devil had made by seduction. These remained * enslaved under the power (of the devil). Moreover, Solomon


[p. 582] {#po.3.p582}
በእንተ ፡ዘገብረ ፡ ሥዕለ ፡ አማልክት ፡ ወኢይመይጦሙ' ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፡ ለእለ ፡ አበሱ ፡ ኅ በ ፡እግዚአብሔር ።፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘወረደ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ሥጋ ፣ እምዘርአ" ፣ ዳዊት" ፣ እማርያም ፡ ድንግል!" ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ውላጤ ፡ በሥነ ፡ መለኮት በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡፡ ወሐመ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተነበ ዩ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡=፡ ወናሁ ፣፤ አማሰነ ፡ ሥልጣኖ" ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ወሰዐ ረ ፤ ጣዖተ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፣፤ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኤር”ም ያስ ፡ ነቢይ ።፡ ናሁ ፤ መዋዕል ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይሜህር' ፡ እንከ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ለአ ሐዱ ፡ እስመ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ያአምሩኒ' ፡ ንኡዕሙ" ፡ ወአቢዮሙ ። ወኢሳይያስኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ይቤ ።፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ውእተ" ፡ ፅለተ ፣ ይትዌከል ፡ ሰብእ ፣ በፈጣሪሁ ፡ ወይኔጽር ፡፣ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱ ሰ፣እስራኤል ፡= ቅዱሰ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወካዕበ” ፡ በእንተ ፡ መንግሥ ተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፡ ይነግሥ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጽዮን ፣፡ ወበኢየሩሳሌም ። ወዓዲ" ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክየ" ፣ አሴብሐከ ፡ ወእነብብ” ፡ ለስምከ ፣ እስመ ፡ ገበርከ” ፡ መ ንክረ” ፡ ግብረ ፣ ወአማን ፡ ኮነ ፡ ”መንክረ ፣ ትካት ፡ አስመ ፡ ረሰይኮን ፣ ለአህጉር ፡መ
sinned, in his old age, in the presence of the Lord, for having made effigies of (false) gods and not having brought back toward the Lord the peoples who were sinning. As for him, he did not become the Word of the Lord, who descended from heaven and took (our) flesh from the lineage of David, from the Virgin Mary, and became man, without change in the beauty of (His) divinity, as the prophet Jeremiah said. (Christ) suffered, as the Law and the prophets prophesied concerning Him. Behold: He annihilated the power of the devil: He abolished the idols and He revealed to the peoples the knowledge of the Lord. As * the prophet Jeremiah said: Behold: days will come: one will no longer teach one another, for all will know me, small and great. The prophet Isaiah said: Thus said the Lord, the God of Israel: That day, man will place (his) trust in his creator, and his eyes will look toward the Holy One of Israel. The Holy One of Israel is Christ. Again, (Isaiah said) concerning the kingdom of Christ: The Lord will reign in Zion and in Jerusalem. Furthermore, he said: (O) Lord, my God, I will glorify you and I will sing your name, for you have done a prodigious work. Truly, (your

ሬተ ፡ ወአውደቀ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ጽኑዓን ፣፡ ወመሠረተ' ፡ ኃጥኣን ፣ ወኢትትሐነጽ" ፡ አህጉ ሪሆሙ ወበእንተዝ ፡ይባርኩከ ፡ አህጉረ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ነዳይ ፣ ወይባርከከ' ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ሰ ብእ ፡ ግሩዓን ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ረዳኤ ፣ ለኩሉ ፣ አህጉር ፣ ትሁት ፣ ወከደንኮሙ ፡ ለእ ለ"ይቴክዙ ፣ በተፅናሶሙ ፡= ኦፍቁራንየ ፤ አኅው" ፣ ናሁ ፡ መጽአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሜጦ ሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፣ እምተቀንዮ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፣ ውስተ"፣0 አምልኮ' ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእ ሙንቱሰ" ፡ ይሴብሕዎ ፡ ወይባርክዎ ፡፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ" ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አ ሕዛብ ፡ እለ ፡ ይትዌከሉ ፡ "ቦቱ ።= እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅያል ፡ ያነስእ ፡ ዐቢየ" ፡ ሰባኬ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ይሰምዑ" ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ለፍቁር ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡፡
work) was * prodigious formerly, for you reduced cities (to) dust and you made the cities of the strong and the foundation of the sinners fall. Their cities will not be (re)built. That is why the cities of the poor people will bless you and the cities of the oppressed men will bless you, for you have become the helper for all the lowered cities and you have sheltered those who are sad, in their destitution. O my dear brothers, behold: Our Lord Jesus Christ has come. He brought men back from the service of the devil to the worship of the Lord. As for them, they will glorify (the Lord) and they will bless Him. As the prophet Isaiah said concerning the peoples, who will place (their) trust * in Him: The Lord is powerful. He will raise up a great preacher in this generation. As for them, they will hear the voice of the heavenly Friend.
ወካክዕበ”"፣ይቤ ፤ ወሀበ” ፡ዘንተ ፣፤ ጸጋ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ።፡ ውእተ አሚረ ፡ይ ቤሉ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወአምላክነ ፡ ዘተሰፈውነ” ፣ ንትፈሣሕ ፡ ወንትሐሠይ” ፡ በ መድኀኒትነ” ፡፡ ወዓዲ" ፡ ይቤ ፡ እአሰምዮሙ ፡ በካልእ” ፡ አስማት ፡=፡ እብል ፡ ቡሩካን”፣ ክርስቲያን ፤ እሙንቱ ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡፡ ናሁ ፡ ኣማስን” ፣ ብሊተ ፣ ሥርዐተ” ፡
Again, he said: (The Lord) gave this grace to all the peoples. That day, one will say: This is our Lord and our God in whom we have hoped. Let us rejoice and exult in our salvation. Furthermore, he said: "I will call them by another name. I will say: Blessed are the Christians who are on the earth. Behold: I will make the Old Testament which is not good disappear, and I will remove it from before my face." See what Our Lord said, by

He said: "(There will be) a New (Testament), because of the people of the Christians who will render worship to the Lord, Most High, and, (trusting) in (His) justice, will believe in Him." But, if it is so, why then do we need to seek the Testament of the Jews and the observation of the Sabbath, and to transgress the commandment of the Lord? Indeed, the prophet Isaiah said concerning the new people, who was chosen from among the peoples: Open the gates; let the people who keep justice, obey righteousness, and protect peace enter! Isaiah said: Wash yourselves, be clean, and take away the malice from your soul from before my eyes, said the Lord. Again, (Isaiah) said: Indeed, (there will be) a spring of water in the desert and * a torrent in a parched region. A forest will grow in the desert. (A spring) will gush forth in a parched land. There will be the festival of birds, in a region of reeds; a forest will be there. The path will be called pure; the path (will be called) holy. Truly, O my dear (brothers), it is a fact that the pure and holy path is the baptism of Christ.

20. The figures of Baptism. - Understand then that Noah and his children were saved and lived through water, and behold, like him, the children of Israel were saved through the red water of the Erythraean Sea. As for Moses, he received holy baptism. It is through water that he was saved and became the son of Pharaoh's daughter. Behold: the prophet preached and revealed concerning the water.

Naaman, the Syrian, also, it is through the water * of baptism that he was purified of his leprosy. But, for us, it has been said (to us) to be baptized in the holy Church of Christ, and to be purified of the defilement of our sins. Thus it was ordered, in the Law, that the priest be washed and be clean. The Lord said, through the mouth of the prophet Isaiah: I myself will blot out sin and I will not remember your fault. Moreover, he said: Do not fear, O my servant Jacob, O my dear Israel, whom I have chosen, for I myself will give water, in their thirst, to those who will go into the desert. I will place my spirit upon your offspring and my blessing will be upon your children. Again, he said: Indeed, I have given water, in the desert, and I have made (water) flow in the desert, * to water my people and my chosen ones whom I have acquired, so that they might set forth my glory.

ancient days. The prophet Joel said: That day, honey will drip from the mountains; milk will flow from the hills * and water will gush forth (in) all the regions of Judah. A spring will gush forth, will come out from the house of the Lord and will feed the river of the Sakino with wisdom.

Have you seen, O my dear (brothers), that it is through water that forgiveness has come and that, by the will (of the Lord), the life (given) by holy baptism was represented in figure? The prophet David said: The Word of the Lord is upon the waters; the God of glory has thundered; the Lord is upon the many waters. Moreover, the Lord said and expressed himself, through the mouth of the prophet Malachi: I am not pleased with you, said the Lord who dominates all. I will not accept the sacrifice from your hand. * From the rising of the sun to its setting, my name will be glorified among the peoples. From all peoples and (all) regions, incense will be brought to my name and a pure sacrifice, for my name will be great among all peoples, said the Lord who dominates all.

21. Rebellion of the sinners. - Know, O (my) brothers, that for those who want to rely on the rule of the Law and the prophets and keep the Sabbath and say that Christ has not yet come, (know and) hear what Isaiah said concerning them: Woe to those who drag their sin, like a long rope, attach, like a yoke of oxen, their iniquity, (and) who say: * Let him act quickly and let us see (his work). The counsel of the Holy One of Israel will come; let us therefore know (it). Woe to those who call evil, good and good, evil, (to those) who call light, darkness and darkness, light, and (to those) who call sweet, bitter and bitter, sweet. Woe to those who are wise for evil and say: We are almost wise. Woe to the strong who drink wine, (and to the) valiant who mix must, absolve the sinner, receive a bribe and make justice bend. That is why, just as straw burns in the coals of fire and is consumed in the flame, so their root will rot and will rise (into the air), like * dust, for they have not taken into account the Law of the Lord Sabaoth. They have angered the Holy One of Israel. The Lord Sabaoth has become angry strongly against his people. He has raised his hand against them and he has

ሥአ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ! ፡፡ ወቀሰፎሙ ፡ ወተምፀ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አድባር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አብድን ቲሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ መሬት ፡ ማእከለ፣ ፍኖት ፡=
chastised them. He became angry against the mountains. The corpses (of his subjects) became like dust in the middle of the road.

Again, (Isaiah) said concerning the unfaithful people of the Jews: (The Lord) said to me: Go to this people and speak to them. They will certainly hear, (but) they will not understand; they will certainly see, (but) they will not know, for the heart of this people has become thick. They have turned away their ears, so as not to hear and they have covered their eyes, so as not to see. (They acted thus), so as not to hear with their ears, not to understand with their intelligence * not to convert, and (so that the
ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፤ ሕዝብ ፡ አብድ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ልበ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ቦሙ ፣ አዕይንተ ፣ ኢይሬ እዩ ፣ወቦሙ ፡እዝነ ፣፡ ወኢይሰምፁዑ ፡፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አነ ፡ ፈጠርኩ ፡ ጥጻ ፡ ለባ ሕር ፡ ከመ፣፤ ኢትኅልፍ " ፡ እምወሰና” ፣ ለዝሉፉ ፡፡ ወካዕበ” ፣ ይቤ ፡=፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ይቀርቡኒ ፡ በከናፍሪሆሙ” ፡ ወበልቦሙሰ ፡ ነዋኅ” ፣ ይርሕቁ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ወከንቶ ፡ ያመልኩኒ” ፡ ወይሜሀሩ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ትእዛዛተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡= ወበእን ተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ናሁ ፡ እደግም ፣ አፍልሶቶሙ" ፡ ለዝንቱ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ ወአፍልሶሙ ፡ ወአህ
Lord) would not show himself merciful toward them. Moreover, he said: (This) people is senseless (and) without intelligence. While they have eyes, they do not see. They have ears and they do not hear. The Lord said: I myself, I created the sand of the sea, so that it would never cross its limit. Again, he said: The Lord said: This people is close to me, by its lips, (but), by its heart, it is strongly distant from me. It is in vain that they render me worship. They teach the doctrine of the commandments of men. That is why, behold: I will begin again

to deport this people, to deport, to make perish the wisdom of the wise and to make forget the counsel of the counselors. Moreover, he said concerning the unfaithful people * of the Jews: Indeed, (it is) a rebellious people (and) of children of falsehood, for the observance of the Law of the Lord (is) not great (among them) who have said to the prophets: Do not announce to us; and to those also who have a vision: Do not expose to us, for you expose to us (hard things), (but) turn us away from the path. Again, he said: Hear, O heaven, and lend ear, O earth, for the Lord has spoken and said: I have begotten children and I have raised (them). As for them, they have revolted against me. The ox has known the one who possesses it, and the donkey (has known) the stable of the house of (its) lord, but Israel has not known me. My people, certainly, has not paid attention to me. Moreover, he said: Woe to the rebels who will have transgressed the Law. Fear, trembling and the net will be * upon those who dwell on the earth. He who will have fled out of fear will fall into the precipice. As for him who will come out of the precipice, the net will seize (him). (My subjects, certainly,) have not turned toward me; they have uttered against me falsehood and vain words, said the Lord.

The prophet Jeremiah said concerning Our Savior Jesus Christ: Behold: they have abandoned me, (me), the source of life. Again, the prophet Jeremiah said: O Lord, make me see, so that I may know and (that) I may fathom their work. As for me, I was like a gentle sheep that one leads, in order to slaughter it, and I did not know the evil counsel that they had decided against me. They said: Let us make his body perish by the wood and let us blot out his life from the earth; let one no longer remember (his) name. (But) you, O powerful Lord, * judge of justice, who probe the heart and the kidneys, render me justice against them. Moreover, Our Lord Jesus Christ said, through the mouth of the prophet David: They have put gall in my food, and they have made me drink vinegar, for (quenching) my thirst. They will be blotted out from the book of the living and they will not be written with your righteous.

As the prophet Daniel said: It is after 69 weeks, after the second construction of the house of the temple that the Christ Savior will come. Thus said (Daniel): While I was speaking, while I was praying, while I was confessing (my) sin and the sin of my people Israel, while I was casting my motive for forgiveness before the Lord, my God, concerning the mountain of the temple of my God, and while I was setting forth my prayer, came the man, Gabriel, whom I had seen in a vision formerly; he was flying. He touched me, at the moment of the sacrifice * of the twilight. He made me understand and conversed with me. He said to me: Daniel, now I have come out, to make you understand wisdom. At the beginning of your prayer the word went out. I have come; I will tell you, for you are a man of desire. Meditate on what you have said and understand what you have seen up to the seventy years, which have been fixed for your people and for your holy city, so that sin might be consumed,

Since the going out of the word * that you have pronounced: (namely that) Jerusalem would be (re)built, until Christ the king, there are 7 years and 62 (years). The squares and the walls will become (built) again and will be (re)built. The days will be exhausted. After 62 years, the anointing will disappear. Judgment will (no longer be rendered) by (the Anointed). The city and the temple will be destroyed by a king who will come; one will cut (them) off by a flood. Continually, one will pierce and kill one another, until the years disappear and (evil) is strong, for many hours. During the half of the week, the altar will be demolished and the corners (of the altar) will be destroyed forever. Quickly, the condition * of many will be destroyed and will become solid (again), in one week. (During) the half of the week, the sacrifice and the offering will be destroyed. Impurity (will exist) in the temple and (will last) until the end of the years; a time will be given for perdition.


[p. 594] {#po.3.p594}

23. Messianic prophecies. - But, from now on, the coming of the prophet, who announces the coming of Christ, is not necessary, for all the prophets, who were sent, began by announcing the coming of Christ. But, if * Christ had not come, the vision of the prophets would not have been sealed. Behold: it is manifest and it is evident that Christ, who was born of the Virgin Mary, in Bethlehem of Judah, is therefore the true Savior. That is why the prophecy ceased and the kingdom of the Jews was abolished. Their sacrifice was destroyed until the ages of ages. Indeed, Christ has come concerning whom the prophets prophesied. He freed the generations of men from sin, and he saved them from the worship of idols and from the hand of the devil, their enemy. Those who were lost he brought back to the knowledge of the Lord, as the prophets prophesied. They announced that * Christ would become the salvation and the life of the whole world.

The city and the temple will be destroyed by a king who will come; this (word) means the king Aristobulus, he whom the Romans took captive with all his people, his children and his brothers and led into their country, as it is written in the book of Denys.

Behold: was accomplished the prophecy of our father, the prophet Jacob, who said: * The dominion will not disappear from Judah, (nor) the principality from his members, until he finds Him who is reserved for him, in the hope of the peoples. After the birth of Our Lord and Our Savior Jesus Christ, our King (and) the Lord of all, in Bethlehem, since then, the kingdom of the Jews was overturned (as well as) the house of the temple. They had (no longer) any sovereign, except only Herod of Rome, who oppressed all the cities of Judah. As for Herod, he was the son of Antestis of Rome. But his mother was Qafarnada, a woman who (was descended) from Arab race. Behold: they have become true the prophecy of our father Jacob and

the prophecy of the prophet Daniel to whom the angel said: Know (it) and understand (it). * Since the going out of the word that you have pronounced: (namely that) Jerusalem will be (re)built and (that its) squares will become (built) again and will be (re)built, the coming of Christ will take place, after 69 weeks, that is to say 423 years. After the (re)construction of the house of the temple and the squares when the 69 weeks had passed, Our Lord Jesus Christ came. He saved us by his holy cross. He made the seduction of Satan fail. He took away the glory (far) from the Jews. He made their priesthood cease and he turned their prophecy (far) from them, as (our father Jacob prophesied). Indeed, in the days of Herod, king of Galilee, who (was part) of the dominion * of Augustus Caesar, king of Rome, was born Our Lord Jesus Christ, the thirty-third year of the days of his royalty from the Virgin Mary, in Bethlehem of Judah. But, if such a (Christ), (O my) brothers, is not himself the Christ concerning whom the prophets prophesied, behold then: our father Jacob lied, he whom the Lord called Israel. (Also lied) Moses, (who) wrote the Law, Daniel, Gabriel, the angel of the Lord, who announced the time (when) Christ would come, and, moreover, the Holy Spirit who spoke through

the mouth of the holy prophets, as (think) those who do not believe in the coming * of Christ, Our Savior, and say: "He has not come."

24. The punishment of the unbelieving Jews. - Behold: was accomplished upon them the prophecy of the prophet Isaiah, who said that they themselves angered the Holy Spirit, accumulated for themselves the curse and distanced themselves from the Holy Spirit. They have remained, until now, 740 years, being afflicted, sad, pushed away, covered with opprobrium and rejected among all the peoples, from the moment when Christ was crucified until this day. Behold: they have been dispersed, they have perished and they have become the servants of the peoples in all the earth, because they did not believe in Christ.
ይቤ ፤ ኢዮስያስ ፣ ጠቢብ ፣፡ ዘተርጐ”መ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አ መ ፡ ይሰቅልቻ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ቦ ፡ ዕደው ፡ እለ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ቃለ ፡ዘይብል ፡ በውሳጤ" ፡ቤ ተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክ ፡ ሖር" ፣ እምዝየ ፡ ወኢትሄሉ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ውስተዝ ፣ መክን" 5፡ ወበይእቲ ፣ ሰዓት ፡ ተሠጠ” ፡ መንጦላዕተ” ፡ ምዙራብ ፡ አምላ ዕሉ ፡ እስከ ፡ ታሕቱ ወእምዝ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ሮም ፡ ወማሀረኩ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወዙሎ፣፣ አድያሚሃ ፡ አውዐዩ ፡ በእሳት” ።
The wise Josiah said that, he whom the prophet Daniel interpreted, * by the Holy Spirit. When (the Jews) crucified Christ, there are men who heard a word saying inside the house of the temple: The Lord God said: Go (far) from here; you will not find yourselves (again) in this place. At that hour, the veil of the temple tore from the top to the bottom. Then, Rome came. It made its prey of the men of Judah and made burn by fire all the surroundings.


[p. 598] {#po.3.p598}

25. Reward of the faithful Jews. - To those who believed in Christ from among the Jews and the others (men) also, before his crucifixion and after his resurrection, the Lord made see miracles. He ordered them to go out from * the house of the temple and to pass, beyond the Jordan, into the land that is called Qalou, so that they might dwell there. After that, the Romans came; they made their prey of the city of Jerusalem and they dispersed (the Jews) to all the ends of the earth. For those who remained in the land that is called Qalou, the Romans did not know their dwelling, for the Holy Spirit had hidden them and had guarded them."
ወእምዝ ፡ አውሥኡ ፡ አኅው ፡ ወይብልዎ ፡ ናሁ ፣ በቀዔተ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ለነ፣ ወበት ምህርትከ" ፡ መንፈሳዊት" ፡ አብራህከ' ፣ አይዕንተ" ፡ አልባቢነ ።፡ ንስአሎ ፣ ለእግዚአ ብሔር" ፡ ይባርክ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፣ ይኩን ፣ በቀዐ” ፡ ትምሀርትከ ፣ እስ”መ ፡ ነበርነ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ዐቢይ" ፡ሐዘን ፡ ወትክዝ” ፡፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ነኅሥሥ ፡ በኀቤከ ፡ ኦእጉየ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ትምህ ርተነ” ፣ ወትወስከነ ፡ እምዝንቱ” ፡ ነገር ፣ መንፈሳዊ ።፡ እስመ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ሰማዕነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰ ቀልዎ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወዘበጥዎ ፣ ወኩርእዎ ፣ ወሞተ ፡ ኢአምነት ፡ ነፍስነ” ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ንጉሠ” ፣ አስራኤል ።፡
26. The passion of Christ prophesied. - Then, the brothers answered and said to him: "Behold: you have become a utility for us; by your spiritual doctrine, you have illuminated the eyes of our hearts. Let us ask the Lord to bless you, so that your doctrine becomes a utility, for * we have remained in great sorrow and (great) sadness. But, now, we will seek from you, our brother Jacob, our doctrine, so that you develop for us this spiritual exposition. Indeed, when we heard that one had crucified Our Lord Jesus Christ, that one had struck him, that one had given him slaps and that he was dead, our soul did not believe that it was the Christ, the king of Israel."


[p. 599] {#po.3.p599}
አውሥአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፣ ዝንቱሰ' ፡ ሕማም ፡ ወመቅሠፍት" ፡ ይኩን ፡ ዘ ሀሎ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ቀዲሙ ፡ ተነግረ ፡ በአፈ ፡ ነቢያት ፡፡ ወበእንቲአሁ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡፡ ናሁ ፣ ይሌቡ ፡ ቀ“ልዔየ ፡ ወይትሌዐል!" ፡ ወይከብር ፡ ወይትፌሣሕ' ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ በከመ ፡ ያነክሩ" ፡ በእንቲአከ ፣ ብዙኃን ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ዘነኪር ፡ ራእዩ ፣ ለሰብእ ፣ ወክብርከ ፡ እምእጓለ ፡ አመሕያው" ።፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ያነክሩ ፣፤ አሕዛብ ፣ ብዙኃን ፣ ወይፈትሑ ፣ አፉሆ ሙ ፣ ነገሥት ፡ እስመ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ኢዜነውዎሙ' ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ያአምሩ" ፤ ሀለዎ ሙ ፡ ወለእለ ፤፡ ኢሰምፅዎ ፣ ይሌብዉ" ፡፡ እግዚኦ ፡ መኑ ፤ ኢመነነ ፡ ቃለነ” ፣ ወለመኑ ፡ ተከሥተ” ፣ መዝራዕተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር። ወነዝነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ሥርው ‹፡ በውስተ ፡ምድር ፡ ጽምእ ት አልቦ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ወአልቦ” ላሕየ ፡ ወራእዩሂ ፡ ምኑን ፡ ወትሑት” ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ሰብ እ ፡ብእሲ ፡ ቅፍ ፤ ውእቱ ፣፤ ወሕሙም ፡ እስመ ፡ ሜጠ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ወአስተሐቀርዎ ፡ ወ ወኢሐሰብዎ ፡፡ እወ ፡ ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ ተዐገሠ” ፡ ሕማመነ ፡ ወጾረ ፡ ኅጣውኢነ ፡ ወተመሰለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ምኑን ፡ ወድኩም ።፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጾ ረ ፡ ኀጣውኢነ ፣ ወበእንቲአነ ፣፤ ሐመ ፤ ወርኢናሁ ፣ ንሕነሂ ፡ ሕሙመ ፡ ወቅሠፈ፡ በ
Jacob answered and said to them: "For these sufferings and (these) blows, which were to take place upon him, they were exposed long ago by the mouth of the prophets. Concerning him, the prophet Isaiah said: Behold: my servant will understand. * He will be raised; he will be illustrious and he will rejoice enormously. Just as many will be astonished concerning you, (for) the aspect (of the Son) of man will be like that of a stranger and your glory will be (far) from the sons of man, (so) many peoples will be astonished at him. The kings will open their mouth, for they will have to know (the things) concerning which one had not announced to them, and they will understand (the things) that they had not heard. O Lord, who has not rejected our word and to whom has the arm of the Lord been revealed? We have exposed (that Christ) was like * a child before (the Lord), and like a root in a parched land. He did not have aspect and he did not have beauty. His aspect was that of a (being) rejected and humiliated by every man. He was a man chastised and pained. Indeed, one turned his face (from him). One despised him and one did not esteem him. Yes, O my dear (brothers), obviously he endured our sufferings, he carried our sins and he became similar to a (being) rejected and weak. As (the


[p. 600] {#po.3.p600}
ሕማም ።፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ቄስለ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ኅጣውኢነ ፡፤-ወሐመ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጌጋይነ ፡ት ምህርተ ፡ ሰላምነ ፣ ወበቀ'ስለ ፡ ዚአሁ ፣ ሐየውነ ፣ ቀስለነ ፣ ከመ ፣ አባግዕ ፣ ተገዲፈነ፡ ብእሷሲኒ ፡ ዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ሖረ ፣ በፍኖቱ ፣ ወስሕተ' ፡ ወመጠዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእን ተ ፡ ኅጣውኢነ ፡፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ኢከሠተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ በሕማሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይነሥአዋ"፣ ለነፍሱ ፣ ወኢያአመርኖዋ ፡ ለልደቱ ፡ ወይነሥእዋ ፡ ለሕይወቱ ፡ በኀጢአተ ፡ ሕዝብየ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ለሞት
prophet Isaiah said: He himself carried our sins and it is because of us that he suffered. For us, we saw him pained and struck by suffering. * Certainly, he was wounded because of our sins and he suffered because of our crime the doctrine of our peace. It is through his own wounds that we were healed of our wounds. When we were lost, like sheep, men went each in their own path and wandered. (Then), the Lord delivered him because of our sins. For him, he did not open his mouth, in his suffering. Indeed, one took his soul; one did not know his birth and one took his life. It is as a result of the sin of my people that he
ለብዉኬ'" ፡- ኦአኀው ፡ ከመ ፡ ሶ"በ ፣ ተሰቅለ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወ ረገዝዎ ፡ ገቦሁ ፤ በኩናት ፣ ወወፅአ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ማይ ፣ ወደም ፡፡ ማይሰ ፡ ትእምርተ፣ ቅድስት" ፡ ጥምቀት ፡፡ ወደምኒ ፤ ውእቱ ፣ ዘይትሜወው ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ለስር የተ ፡ ኅጢአት ፡፡ ወቦ ፣ ኅበ ፣ ይቤ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ኩናት ፡ ዘረገዝዎ ፣ ገቦሁ =፡ ውእተ ፡ አሚረ ፤ ሀለዎሙ ፡ ይርአይዎ ፡ ለዘ ፣ ወግእዎ = ዘውእቱ ፡ ዳግም" ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወ መድኀኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ በዓቢይ ፣ ክብር ፣ ወስብሐት" ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰራዊተ ፡ መ ላእክት ፤ ይኩንን' ፡ ሕያዋነ ፣ ወሙታነ ፡ ወይሬእይዎ ፡ እለ ፡ ክህድዎ ፡ ወይበክዩ ፡
reached death. Understand then, O my brothers, that when * Our Lord Jesus Christ was crucified and his side was pierced with a lance, water and blood came out of it. The water is the sign of holy baptism. As for the blood, it is what the people of the Christians receive for the remission of sins. Somewhere, (Scripture) said regarding the lance with which his side was pierced: On that day, they will see Him whom they have pierced, that is to say (at) the second (coming) of Our Lord and of Our Savior Jesus Christ, in great honor and (great) glory, with the armies of the angels, * to judge the living and the dead. Those who have denied him will see him. They


[p. 601] {#po.3.p601}
መሪረ ፡ ወትግፅር ፣ ነፍሶሙ' ፣ በኅበ ፣ ኢይበቀዕ ፣ ወኢምንተኒ ፣ ወይከውን ፣ ክፍ ሎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ኔኔ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡፡
will weep bitterly and their soul will cry out, while that will be of no use. Their portion will be (to find themselves) in eternal punishment."
አውሥኡ ፡ አኅው ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡፤ ኦእተየ ፡ ያፅቆብ እይ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘተነበየ ፣ በእን ተ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ወትንሣኤሁ ፤ እሙታን ።፡ ተሰጥወ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትቤ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ሞቱ ፣ ከመዝ ጥሕረ ፤ ከመ ፡፣ ዐንበሳ" ፡ ወከመ ፡- እጓለ ዐንበሳ ፣ ዘይነብር ፣ ተኅቢኦ ፣ "ወመኑ ፣ ይከልኦ ፡ ንዒወ ፡ ወዳዊትኒ ፡ነቢይ ፡ ይቤ ፡= እስመ ፣ ኢተኅድጋ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ለነፍስየ" ፡=፡ ወኢትሁቦ ፡ ለጻድቅከ" ፡ ይርአ ይ፣ሙስና ፡ ወዕበ ፣ ተቀብረ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘህልው ፡ ምስሌሁ ፤ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፣ ውስተ ፤ መቃብር ፡ ወረደ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ወፈ ትሐ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ሙቁሓን' ፡ በውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ወአብጠለ ፣፡ ሥልጣኖ" ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፤ ጸላኢ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ዳዊት-፡ ነቢይ ፡=፡ ፈነወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ወአሕየዎሙ ፡፡ ወአድኅኖሙ ፡ እሞቶሙ ፡ ወካዕበ ፣ ይቤ ፣ ዐውየ"ዉ ፡ ኅበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ሶበ፣ ተመንደቡ ። ወአድኅኖሙ” ፣ እምንዳቤሆሙ ። ወአውጽኦሙ ፣ እምጽልመት ፣ ወጽላ ሎተ ፡ ሞት ፡፡ ወሰበረ ፡ መዋቅሕቲሆሙ” ፡፡
27. The death of Christ prophesied. - The brothers answered and said to him: "O my brother Jacob, which prophet prophesied about the death of Our Lord and his resurrection from the dead?" Jacob answered and said: "The holy Law said such (words) about his death: He roars like a lion and like a lion's cub that remains hidden. * And who will prevent him from lying in wait (for his prey)? The prophet David said: Indeed, you will not abandon my soul in Sheol and you will not allow your Just One to see corruption. 28. The souls delivered from Sheol. - When the body of Our Lord Jesus Christ, with which the Word of the Lord (co)exists, was buried, and they had placed him in the tomb, (Christ) descended into Sheol, delivered the souls of the prisoners in Sheol, and annihilated the power of the devil, the enemy. That is why the prophet David said: The Lord sent his Word. He made (men) live and saved them from their death. Again, he said: They lamented * to the Lord, when they were tormented. He saved them from their torment. He brought them out of darkness and the shadow of death and he broke their chains.

Furthermore, (David) said: Indeed, he broke the door of bronze and he smashed the bars of iron. The prophet Isaiah said: Indeed, his soul was delivered to death and he was counted with the sinners. Certainly, he took upon (himself) the sin of many and it is because of their sin that he himself was delivered. Again, he said: (I have spoken) to my Messiah, (to) Cyrus, whom I have taken in my hand, in order to make the peoples hear before him, * to raise the strength of kings, to open the doors before him, and (so that) the cities would not be closed. Furthermore, he said: I will put (the tomb of) the wicked in place of his tomb, and (the death of) the rich in place of his death. Indeed, he did not commit sin, and deceit was not in his mouth. But he loved humility. Again, he said: He will say to the captives: Come out; and to those who are in the shadow of death: See the light. As for the death of Our Lord Jesus Christ, it is salvation. Indeed, it is because of the transgression and the fault of Adam that all the souls * of the good and the wicked remained chained in Sheol, under the power of the devil, the enemy. (But), in his considerable clemency and his

souls and he made them live. 29. The prayers of the prophets. - The prophets, although they had seen that Adam and his children had been defeated, they whom the devil had made captive *—who had been powerful over them—could not save them and they could not heal them (from his attacks). For their part, they remained, praying, asking, day and night, of the Lord and saying: "O Lord, have pity on your servants and show yourself merciful toward them. Hear their request. May our prayer penetrate before you. Do not turn your face from us, for we are tormented enormously. The enemy has defeated us. But you, O Lord, help us and save us by your holy name." The prophet David said: O Lord, incline your heavens and descend toward us. Isaiah * said: Awake, O Lord, why do you sleep? The Word of the Lord, who does not sleep and does not die, raised to himself our own image

in (his) soul and (his) flesh. That is why the prophet David said: The Lord will rise and his enemies will be scattered. His enemies will flee from before his face. Behold: he will save the souls that are in Sheol, from Adam until the coming of Christ. It is about them that the prophet David said: You have ascended into the heights, having taken captivity captive; you have given your grace to the sons of man. Indeed, when Our Lord Jesus Christ * had ascended onto the tree of the cross and had stretched out his holy hands, he defeated Satan and he took captivity captive, (taking it) from the hands (of Satan). The prophet Isaiah said: As for those who dwell in darkness and the shadow of death, a great light has risen for them.

30. The plot against Christ. - Have you seen, O (my) brothers, the wonder, which occurred for the salvation of the children of men, (wrought) by Our Lord Jesus Christ? As the prophet said: Behold: the Just One will perish and no one will be sad about him. The strong without number will gather and will destroy the Just One by (their) iniquity. (But) his resting place will become glorious. Again, the prophet Isaiah said * about the death of Christ and also (about) those who were crucified with him: Indeed, his soul

ለሞት ፤ ነፍሱ ፣ ወተጐለቄ ፣ ምስለ ፣ ኃጥኣን' ፣ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ያዐርግ'፣ ኀጢአቶሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኀጢአቶሙ ፣ ተውሕበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ በእንተ ፣፤ ሕዝብ ፡ አለ ፣ ሰቀልዎ" ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡፡ ንእስ ሮ ፡ ለጻድቅ" ፡ እስመ ፡ ክቡድ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ለነ ፡።፡ ወይትቃወሞ ፡ ለምግባሪነ ፡፡ ወይዘነኾ ጐነ ፡ በኅጢአት" ፡ ለሕግ ፡፡ ወይሰብክ ፡ ለነ ፣ አበሳ ፡ ትምህሀርትነ ፡፡ ወይብል ፤ አእም ሮ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ ብየ ፡ "ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይብል ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ኮነነ ፡ መዝለፌ" ኅሊናነ' ፍ፡ ክቡድ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ለነ ፡ በራእዩ ፡፡ እስመ ፡ ኢይትመሰል ፡ ምስለ ፡ ባዕዳን" ፡ ንብረቱ ፡ ወፍሉጥ ፤ ፍኖቱ ፡=፡ ምኑናነ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ ወይርሕቅ ፣፤ እምፍናዊነ ፡ ከመዘ ፡ እምርኩስ ፡፡ ወያስተበጽዕ ፣ ደኃሪቶሙ ፣ ለጻድቃን" ፡፡ ወይትሜክካሕ ፡ በአብ ፣፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ንርአይ ፡ እመ ፡ ነገሩ ፣ ህልው = ወናመክር ፣፡ ደኃሪቶሙ" ፡፡ ወእመሰ ፣ ጻድ ቅ ፡ ወልዱ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ያድኅኖ ፣ ወይባልሆ ፡ እምእለ ፡ ይትቃወምዎ ፡፡
was delivered to death and he was counted with the sinners. For his part, he took upon (himself) the sin of many, (and) it is because of their sin that he himself was delivered. The prophet Jeremiah said about the people who crucified Christ: Let us bind the Just One, for he is burdensome to us. He opposes our work. He ridicules us because of the sin of the Law. He preaches to us the fault of our doctrine. He says: I have the knowledge of the Lord. He calls himself * the Son of the Lord. He has become for us a (being) refuting our thoughts. He is burdensome to us by his appearance. Indeed, his way does not resemble (that of) others and his manner is apart. We have become for him (beings) to be rejected. He distances himself from our ways, as if he (were distancing himself) from impurity. He proclaims blessed the end of the just. He boasts (of having) the Lord for Father. Let us see if his word is true. Let us test his end. But, if this Just One is the Son of the Lord, (the Lord) will save him and will deliver him from those who will oppose him.

the cause of the malice that remained upon them and of the error that blinded their hearts, they did not know the mysteries of the Lord. As for those who rose against him, fought against him, and accused him before the people, they are the priests who are of the lineage of Levi and the pharisees who are of the lineage of Simeon. These are the two lineages that associated themselves, in order to crucify Our Lord * Jesus Christ. The wrath (of the Lord) remained upon them. As (our father, the prophet Jacob, prophesied and said) by the grace of the Holy Spirit: Simeon and Levi are brothers. They brought about iniquity, in their impetuosity, with their lance. May my soul not join with them and may it not descend into their tumult, for, in their anger, they killed seven (men) and, (in) their desire, they cut the nerves of the city-dwellers. Cursed be their anger, for they fear (nothing, in) their indignation. I will divide them in Jacob and I will disperse them in Israel.

But, if there are (people) * among the Jews who say: "Jacob said this about Dinah, his daughter," we will say: "It is not as you say." Indeed, Jacob did not curse them because they took vengeance on the impure, who had uncovered the shameful parts of their sister. Far be it that Jacob, the Blessed, cursed those who took vengeance

on the impure. However, Jacob knew, by the Holy Spirit, the great sin that would come out of their descendants. He prophesied about them what will happen in the last days, how they are of the lineage of Levi, the priests, * who rose against Christ, and they are of the lineage of Simeon, the pharisees, who accused (Christ), in order to crucify him.

Behold: the prophecy of Jacob has been fulfilled upon them, who said: My soul did not take pleasure (in) their dwelling, for he himself knew, by the Holy Spirit, what the priests and the pharisees would do against Christ. That is why he uttered a curse against them. The prophet Isaiah said about their person: Indeed, they deliberated an evil counsel against his soul; they said: Let us bind the Just One, for he is hard and burdensome to us. But, now, they eat (their) harvest, (as) food. Woe to the * sinner in his malice. He will find according to the malice of the work of his hands.

prophesied Jacob, Isaiah, and other prophets too. The prophet said: Behold: the iniquitous will rise, who meditate evil, insult the Most High, and speak iniquity enormously. The life (of Christ) will appear (from the top of) the wood. From (his) body will flow water and blood. The Word of the Lord will be heard. He will remain on the wood, (for) three hours. * He will be placed in the tomb, when he is dead. He will rise from the dead. His (new) life will be manifested. He will ascend from the earth into heaven. He will not perish, (but) he will live forever. He will be seated at the right hand of the Most High. He will rest on the Cherubim. He existed originally, for he himself was existing from the beginning; originally he was begotten. The saints announced about him. They themselves were sad with those who followed them. The peoples rose against them.
ርኢክሙኑ ፡ ኦአኅው” ፣ ዘከመ ፡ ፈከረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ ስቅለቶ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወ ትንሣኤሁ ፡ እሙታን ፡ ወፅርገቶ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወንብረቶ ፣ በየማነ” ፣ አብ ወ ዘከመ ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ህልው ፡ እምትካት ፡፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ነገረ ፡ በእን ተ ፡ አርድእት ፣፡ ወእለሂ ፣ ይተልውዎሙ”" ፣ ዘይመጽእ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሐዘን ፣ ወምንዳ ቤ ፡ ለአለ” ፡ አምኑ ፣ በክርስቶስ ፡፡ ወክከዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ስቅለቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ ወ
Have you seen, O (my) brothers, how the prophet explained the crucifixion of Christ, his resurrection * from the dead, his ascent into the heavens, his session at the right hand of the Father, and (explained) how he rests on the Cherubim (and) how he is existing from the beginning? Furthermore, he exposed, about the disciples and those also who will follow them, the oppression and the torment that will come upon those who will have believed in Christ. You have brought me to the tribunal and you have delivered me, to be crucified on the wood. * Have you understood that this exposition, which our fathers made about Christ, is luminous and evident?" 33. The resurrection of Christ prophesied. - The brothers answered and said to him: "Explain to us which book mentions the resurrection of Our Lord Jesus Christ." Jacob answered and said to them: "Behold: we will begin by explaining all this. The prophet Hosea said: Let us go and return to the Lord, our God, for he himself has chastised us, (but) he will heal us. He has wounded us, (but) he will give us the remedy. He will make us live the second day. The third day, we will rise and we will live before him. Again, he said: Where is your punishment, O death, and * where is your sting, O Sheol? The prophet Zechariah said: By virtue of the blood of (my) Testament I have delivered your prisoners from the pit without water.
ዳዊትኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ እግዚኦ ፣ ወኩንና ፣ ለምድር ።፡፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትወርስ ፣ በኩሉ" ፡ ምድር ፡፡ ወክዕበ ፣ ይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡፣ ኢተኅድጋ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ሲኦል ፡

The prophet said: The Lord is pleased to humble himself, to remove the bonds of (his) servants, to bear their sin, in order to give salvation to the peoples, and to destroy the strong on the earth. It is because of us that he delivered his soul to death * and that he was counted with the sinners.

34. False interpretation of prophecies. - The brothers said to him: "(O) Jacob, you have not understood this exposition, for our doctors and our elders say (that) the prophet gave this exposition about King Josiah." Jacob answered and said to them: "They have lied; it is not so." They answered and said to him: "Make us understand and explain to us (what) made them err." Jacob said to them: "King Josiah was not crucified like a bandit; he was not counted with the sinners; he did not remove sin (away) from the race of men; he did not give salvation to the peoples; he did not destroy the strong on the earth and he did not deliver his soul * (in) redemption (for) all. But only, (there were enemies), who took his people captive and made them (their) prey; but he, he could not save (his people). Now, Our Lord Jesus Christ was crucified between bandits; he was counted with the sinners; he was placed in the tomb and he rose again, the third day. The peoples, having seen his resurrection from the dead, believed in


[p. 611] {#po.3.p611}

him. When they had believed, he saved them from their sin. He annihilated the impurity of the (false) gods. The souls that had remained chained in Sheol, the posterity of Adam, the prophets, the good and the faithful, he redeemed them and he brought them out of Gehenna.

* The prophet Isaiah said about the heavenly Father: Behold: a just king will reign. As for (his) leaders, they will judge with justice. I myself will establish justice and righteousness in all his paths. That (day), he will (re)build my city and he will bring back the captives of my people, not by a ransom and not by a bribe, said the Lord Sabaoth. Thus said the Lord Sabaoth: Egypt and the cities of Ethiopia will be overwhelmed. Distant men will come toward you. They will be enslaved to you and they will follow you (from) behind while they will be chained (by) the hands; they will prostrate themselves before you and they will pray to you. For the Lord is with you. There is no other * Lord than you. You are the (only) Lord. We have recognized you (as) the life-giving God (Thus said) the prophet.

35. Uselessness of the sabbath. - The brothers answered and said to him: * "The Lord knows that you have said a true (word) and that you have uttered a right (word). But, from now on, it is not fitting that we be sorrowful and that we be sad to have been baptized and to have become Christians. But, if we keep the sabbath, that will not be useful to us before Christ." Jacob said to them: "O (my) brothers, behold: the people remained long ago, before the law of Moses, in the Testament of the flesh. (The Israelites) did not have commandments, nor the observation of the sabbath. However, when the law of Moses came, (Moses) ordered them to keep the sabbath and all the commandments. (But), after Our Lord Jesus Christ came, * the Sun of justice, we must not abandon him and go in the way of the observation of the sabbath.

However, the Testament of Christ is greater and more glorious than it, seventy times, (the Testament of Christ), that is to say the holy Gospel *.

for the Law will come forth from me. Indeed, my judgment will be the light of the peoples. My justice will come quickly and my salvation will arrive. The peoples will place (their) trust in my arm. The peoples and the Isles will hope in me and will place (their) trust in my arm * The prophet Micah said: In the last days the New Testament will arise. The mountain of the Lord will appear splendid on the mountain tops and will rise above the hills. The peoples will run toward it and many peoples will go toward it and say: Come, let us go up to the mountain of the Lord and (enter) into the house of the God of Jacob, so that he may show us his way and that we may walk in his path. Indeed, (it is) from Zion (that the Law will come), and the Word of the Lord (will come) from Jerusalem.

36. The New Testament. - Have you seen, (O my) dear (brothers), what the prophets said: (namely), that after the Law of Moses, another law will come. Behold then: the New Testament of Our Lord Jesus*-Christ has come, that is to say, the holy Gospel which the prophets called the Light. We must not live by the Law of the Jews, nor keep the Sabbath, so that we do not become the enemies of the Lord and the adversaries

of the prophets, as we (said formerly). As Moses said: Woe to the man who will not listen to the word of the prophet who will come in my own name. I myself will rebuke him before the Lord. The prophet Malachi said: I am not pleased with you, said the Lord who dominates all, and I will (not) accept the sacrifice (coming) from your hand. Indeed, from the rising of the sun to the * setting, my name will be glorified among the peoples, and from all the peoples and from (all) the regions incense will be brought to my name and a pure sacrifice, for great is my name among all the peoples, said the Lord who dominates all.

The prophet David said: Hear me, (O) my people. I will speak to you, (O) Israel, (and) I will testify (the truth) to you. As for me, I am God and your God. It is not because of the sacrifice that I will rebuke you. Indeed, your offering is before me at all times. I will not take oxen (coming) from your house, nor goats (coming) from your flock, for all the beasts of the desert are mine (as well as) the animals of the mountains and the oxen. I know all the birds of the sky. As for the beauty of the plain, it is with me. Even if * I am hungry, I will not ask you, for the whole world in its fullness is mine. I do not eat the flesh of the ox and I do not drink the blood of the goat. Offer to the Lord a sacrifice of glory and send him your vows.

37. Sunday. - Have you noticed, (O my) brothers, how it was explained regarding the consumption of the Law by the command of the merciful Lord? But, from now on, it is (not) right that we keep the day of the Jews, (but it is right) that we keep the holy Sabbath of Sabbaths, that is the perfect feast of the resurrection of Our Lord Jesus-Christ, for it is the great day of the Lord that he created first. It was called "Light," so that we might rejoice in it. As David said: Let us rejoice and exult in it. (It is) not the Sabbath of the Jews (which) is the last of the days. (But) Sunday is * the light of the world, (the day) in which Our Lord Jesus-Christ rose from the dead and (in which) the Sun of justice rose for (the

men). 38. The Eucharist. - The prophet Isaiah said regarding the body and blood of Our Lord, that is to say, the Eucharist: I saw with my eyes the Lord Sabaoth, the King. One of the Seraphim was sent to me. In his hand was a coal that he had taken with tongs from the altar. He made it touch my lips and he said to me: Behold: I have made your lips touch (with) this (coal). I have taken away your sins and your faults; it has purified you. Indeed, it is thus that the priests eat of this bread.
