As for Melchizedek, (a word) which (means) in its interpretation King of peace, * he persevered in offering before the Lord bread and wine, that is to say, the image of the mystery of the Church of Christ the Savior, in which bread and wine are offered. Indeed, our fathers ate the bread of heaven, that is to say, the image of the mystery of the Eucharist. But those who ate the bread of manna, which came down from heaven, all died.

The Eucharist is the bread that Our Lord Jesus-Christ gave (for) the salvation of the whole world. Through it he made the light rise and he brought (men) back from error to true worship. (Through it) the spiritual (beings) will live forever. * No one will say anymore to his brother, nor to his friend: Come, let us go up (to) the mountain of the Lord. Indeed, the food is for all; the knowledge of the Lord is like a considerable water and has become a spiritual river, which flows into a great sea. Isaiah said: The Lord Sabaoth has decreed to abolish the reproach of the illustrious and to confound all the great ones of the earth.

Again, the prophet Isaiah said: * Thus said the Lord, the God of Israel: That day, (the people) will place (their) trust in their Creator and their eyes will look toward the Holy One of Israel. (The Israelites) will no longer have trust in their idols, (nor) in the work of their hands, (nor in that) which their fingers will have made. The prophet Zechariah said: Many peoples will come and many peoples (will arrive), seeking the face of the Lord who dominates all, in Jerusalem, and (beseeching) the face of the Lord. Again, he said: (It is) thus (that) it will happen. That day, the names of the idols will disappear from the whole earth and no one will remember them anymore.

40. The betrayal of Judas. - The brothers said to him: "Behold: we have noticed and we have understood that everything you have said is true. Indeed, it is (inspired) by the Holy Spirit what * your mouth has uttered. But now, explain to us why Christ was crucified and was killed because of Judas, his disciple." Jacob answered and said to them: "Yes, (my) dear (brothers), the holy prophets announced in advance that the Word of the Lord would become incarnate and that he would take his body from us. Again, they preached everything that would happen to him."


[p. 619] {#po.3.p619}
ይቤ ፡ አሞጽ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ትከይድዎ፤' ፡-ለጸድቅ ፡ ወትነሥኡ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ ወኤርምያስኒ ፡ ነ ቢይ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ ሜጦ ፡ ለክቡር ፡ ቋብሩረ ፡ ዘአክበርዎ ፣ እምደቂቀ ፣ እስራኤል ፣ ወወሀብዎ ፡ ሜጦ.፣ በገራህተ ፡፣ ለብሓዊ" ፡ ወኢሳይያስኒ ፣ ይቤ ፣ አሌ ፣ ሎቱ" ፡ ለኃጥ እ ፡ በአከዩ ፡ ምግባረ ፡፣ አደዊሆሙ ፡ ይረክብ ። ወዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሲም ፡ ላዕ ሴሁ ፡ ኃጥአ 5፡ “ወሰይጣን ፡ ይቁም ፡ በየማኑ ፡=፡ ወሶበሂ ፣ ይትዋቀሥ ፡ ይፃእ ፣፡ ተመዊ ኦ ፡ ወጸሎቱሂ ፡ ትኩኖ ፣ ጌጋየ ፡፡ ወይኩና ፣ መዋዕሊሁ ፣ ሕዳጠ ። ወሲመቶሂ ፣ይን ሳአ ፡ ባዕድ።
The prophet Amos said: You trample the Just and you take ransoms. As for the prophet Jeremiah, he said: They took as the price of the Glorious one thirty (pieces of) silver, (he) whom they valued on the part of the children of Israel and gave it (as) the price of the potter's field. Isaiah said: Woe to the sinner. In his malice he will find the work of his hands. The prophet David said: Set a sinner against him. * Let Satan stand at his right hand. And also, when he is on trial, let him go out, being defeated. Let his prayer even become a crime for him. Let his days become few. As for his office, let another take it.
ለበውክሙኑ ፡ ኦአኅው ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ አስቆ ሮታዊ ፡ ከመ! ፡ ሰማዕነ ፣ ከማሁ ፡ሶበ ፡ነስአ ፣ ኅብስተ ፣ ወፅአ፣ ወአግብኦ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ተሰደ ፡ ወነስአ ፡ ሲመቶ ፡ ባዕድ ።=፡ ይቤ፣ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ በእንተ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡፡ ብእሲ ፣ አብድ ፡ ወዓማዊ ፡ የሐ ውር ፡ ፍናወ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ጌራተ ።፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይቀጽብ ፡ በዐይን ፡ ወይኤምር ፡ በእግ ር = ወይሜህር ፡ በቅጽበተ ፡ አጻብእ ፡፡ ወዕሉት" ፡ ልብ ፡ ትሔሊ ፡ እኩየ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊ ዜ ፡=፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ሀከከ ፡ ያመጽእ ፡ ለሀገር ።፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ግ”ብተ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኅጐለቱ፣
Have you not understood, O (my) brothers, how the prophet David prophesied, regarding Judas Iscariot, the same things that we have heard, when he took the bread, went out and delivered the Lord Jesus-Christ. As for him, he was expelled and another took his office. Solomon, son of David, said regarding that disciple: The foolish and iniquitous man goes (in) paths that are not good. He himself makes signs with his eye, points with his foot and teaches with the sign of his fingers. (His) perverse heart meditates evil at all times. Such a (being) brings tumult upon the city. That is why, suddenly * his ruin will come, (as well as) the blow and the breaking

Yes, (O my) dear (brothers), this disciple gave a sign to the Jews; he spoke with them, so that they might seize Christ upon a sign (of the head) and he said to them: The one to whom I myself will say: Peace to you, and the one whom I will kiss, seize him. Understand, O (my) brothers, that the fact that the brothers of Joseph sold the son of Jacob is the image of Our Lord Jesus-Christ who was sold by the Jews. These are his brothers by the flesh. The Lord said: Cursed be he who receives a bribe, in order to kill the blood of the pure soul; and all the people said: Amen. Amen. Behold: it has been accomplished * the prophecy of Moses regarding the one who received a bribe and delivered Christ. Again, he said: The Lord will not want to forgive him. But only, the punishment of the Lord, which will grow upon the word (of the Lord) and which (the Lord) will make remain upon him, will come upon him. As it is written in the Scripture: His name will be blotted out from under the heaven.

41. Repudiation of the Jews. - The brothers answered and said to him: "Truly, (O) Jacob, you have prophesied to us a right (thing) and you have elucidated a hidden exposition. But now, reveal to us why the Lord rejected our people." Jacob answered and said to them: "The prophet Isaiah said:


[p. 621] {#po.3.p621}
ብሔር ፡ ለሕዝብነ ፡፡ አውሥአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ከመ ዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፀባኦት ፡ ሖር ፡ ቤተ ፡ ስምናን ፣ ወልደ ፣ ጥምያ ፡ወበሎ፣ ምንተአ ፡ "መጻእከ ፡ ወምንተአ ፡ ተሐበልከ ፡ በዝየ ፡ ትግበር ፡ መቃብረ ፡ ወአውቀር ከ ፣ ዝህረ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ኩክሕ፤' ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ወግር ፡ ወገበርከ ፡ ለከ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ናሁ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ጸባኦት ፣ ያወጽእሂ ፣ ወይቀጠቅጥሂ ፡ ብእሴ ፣ ወይሰልበከ ፣ አልባሲከ ፡ ወ አክሊለ ፡ ክብርከ ፡፡ ወይገድፈከ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘኢትትጐለቀ፦" ፡ ወትመው ት ፡ በሀየ ፡ ወያኅስሮ ፡ ለሰረገላከ ፡ ሠናይ ፣ ወይከይድዋ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ መላእክቲከ ፡ ወትሰ ዐር ፣ እምምግብከ! ፡ ወእምምኩናኒከ ፡፡ ወትወፅእ ፡ እምክብርከ ፡፡ ትርጓሜሁ ፡ ለስም
Thus said the Lord Sabaoth: Go to the house of Shebna, son of Temya, and say to him: Why * have you come? Why have you dared to make a tomb here, have you dug a sepulcher in the rock that is in the hill and have you made a house for yourself? Behold: the Lord Sabaoth will make you go out and will break you, (you) man. He will strip you of your garments and of the crown of your glory. He will throw you into the great immeasurable sea and you will die there. He will debase your beautiful chariot. One will trample the house of your ministers. You will be dismissed from your administration and from your dominion and you will go out from your glory. The interpretation of Shebna means "chased out".
ናን ፡ ስዱድ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ብሂል ፡=፡ ርኢክሙኑ"፣ አኀው ፡ ከመ ፡ትሰሀዐር ፡ ህገ ፣ አይሁድ ፣ ወተፈጸመ ፡ በአድሜሁ ፡፡፡ ወዓዲ ፡ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይተሉ ፡ ቃ”ለ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ምጽአቱ ፣ ለክርስቶስ ውእተ ፡ አሚረ ፡ እጹውዖ ፡ ለቀ'ልኡየ ፡ ኤልያቂም ፣ ወልደ ፡ ኪልቅዩ ፣ ወኣለብሶ"፣ አልባሲከ ፡ ወእሁቦ ፡ አክሊለከ ፡ ወኣሜግቦ ፣ ምግበከ ፣ ወኣኬቤንኖ' ፡ ምኩናኒከ ፡ ወኣገ ብእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፣ ወይከውኖሙ ፡፣ አቡሆሙ ፡ ለእለ ፣ ይነብሩ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወእ ሁቦ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወይቤንን ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘየአብዮ ፣ ተኩንኖ ፡ ወእሰይም ፡ መልአ
Have you seen, (O my) brothers, that the Law of the Jews was abolished and was consumed in its time? Furthermore, Isaiah said, continuing his word, * regarding the coming of Christ: That day, I will call my servant Eliakim, son of Hilkiah. I will clothe him with your garments and I will give him your crown. I will charge him with your administration; I will defer your dominion to him and I will put (your power) into his hand. He will become the father of those who will dwell in Judah; I will give him the glory of David. He will dominate and no one will refuse him obedience. I will make him prefect of a faithful region. I will place him

Thus said the Lord Sabaoth: Let the man who is standing in a strong region stagger. One will also remove * and his glory will fall upon him, for the Lord has spoken thus. The prophet Amos said: In the name of the Lord, who dominates all, behold: the glory of Jacob will be abolished. I have hated his work. I will deliver the city because of him.

42. The crucifixion of Christ. - Have you (re)cognized, (O my) brothers, that salvation will come through Christ, son of the living Lord. Behold then: it is fitting that we celebrate the great day of the Lord, the holy day of Sunday, (for) the resurrection of Our Lord Jesus-Christ is a joy for the whole world. Let us not go into the Law of the Jews that the Lord hated, as he said (it) through the mouth of the prophet that we first mentioned." The brothers said to him: * "You have spoken true, (O) our brother. Behold: we have known and we have noticed the death of Christ. However, we will question you, so that you may explain to us why Christ died in a harsh torment."

43. The bronze serpent, figure of the cross. - Again, (Moses) said in the Law: The Lord sent upon the people the serpent of the earth which killed and bit the people. Many people died. Again, he said: The Lord said to Moses: Make yourself a serpent. Place it (there) where they will give a sign. Every time the serpent of the earth will have bitten a man, let every (being) who will have been bitten see it; he will live. Indeed, (the serpent) became the image and the sign of the cross of Christ. The Lord said to Moses: Suspend the bronze serpent on the wood, crosswise. Just as (Moses) suspended the serpent of the earth, in the desert, as an image of the cross, and (this one) became the salvation * for the people of Israel against the bite of the serpents, in the same way the cross of Christ is the salvation for every (being) who will believe in it, (salvation) against the bite of the devil, the great serpent.

44. Other figures of the cross. - There is another sign of the cross. Indeed, when Jacob blessed the children of Joseph, Ephraim and Manasseh, he placed (his hands) on them, in the shape of a cross. It is through it that the blessing will come. As for the prophet Moses, he struck the sea with his long staff, he split the sea, and the troops of the children of Israel passed. After the armies of Pharaoh had followed (them) from behind, the Lord ordered Moses to strike the sea a second time with his staff, and the sea covered the enemies (of Israel) upon the sign of the cross. * Again, when Moses was fighting with Amalek, while he was at the top of the mountain, he stretched out his hands in the shape of a cross. Having seen this sign, (the people of) Amalek fled from before the face (of Israel. But), when (Moses) had lowered his hands (and) had removed the sign of the cross, immediately (the people of) Amalek returned toward (Israel) and had the upper hand. The prophet Jeremiah said regarding the people who crucified Christ: Come: let us destroy his flesh by the wood; let us blot out his life from the earth and let us not remember (anymore) his name. Again, he said regarding Our Lord Jesus-Christ: As for me, I was like a gentle lamb that one leads, in order to slaughter it, and I did not know the evil counsel that they

ወይቤ”"ሉ ፡ ንፁ ፡ ንደይ ፡ ዕፀ ፣ ውስተ ፣ ኅብስት ፣ ወንቀጥቅጦ ፣ እምድረ' ፣ ሕያዋ ዓ ወዳዊትኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ቀነዉኒ ፡ እደውየ ፣ ወእገርየ ፡= ወጐለቁ" ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አዕጽ ምትየ ፡= አሙንቱሰ ፡ ጠየቆሙ ፡ ተዐወሩኒ ። ወተካፈሉ ፡ አልባስየ ፡ ለርአሶሙ ፡= ወተዓፀዉ' ፡ ዲበ ፡ አራዝየ ፡=፡ ወይቤ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፣ ነቢይ ፣ ወኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ እሠፍ ሕ ፡ እዴየ ፣ ላዕለ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ አላውያን ፣ ወከሓድያን'"።፣ እርአይ ፡ ለእመ ፡ የኅፍሩ"፣ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ኢኅፈሩኒ' ፡ ወኢሖሩ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ጽጽቅ ፣ ወተለወቶሙ ፣ሐጢአቶሙ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ዕልው ፣ አምዕዑኒ ፡ ወሠርዑ ፡ ማእደ ፡ ለአማልክት ፡ ወዐጠኑ ፡ ለአጋ ንንት ።=
had deliberated against me. They said: * Come, let us put wood in his bread, and let us remove him from the land of the living. The prophet David said: They nailed my hands and my feet, and they counted all my bones. As for them, they observed them (and) they disdained me. They divided my clothes among themselves, (to attribute them) to themselves, and they cast lots upon my garment. The prophet Isaiah said: All day long, I stretch out my hand to a rebellious and unfaithful people, in order to see if they will revere me. As for them, they do not revere me. They do not walk (in) the path of justice. Their sin follows them. This people is perverse. They make me angry. They set the table for the (false) gods, and they offer incense to the demons.
አውሥኡ ፡ አኅው ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ዕፁበ ፣ ነገርከ ፡ ወሠናየ ፡ ነበብከ ፡= እምይእዜ ሰ ፡ይደ"ሉ ፡ ንሰግድ ፣ ለመስቀል ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወናአብዮ ፣ በዘቦቱ ፡ ተሰቅለ ፣ እግዚእ ነ ፣፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ተሰጥወ" ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ እወ ፡ አኅው ፡ ርቱዕ ፡ ለነ፣፡ና ዕብዮ ፡ ወናክብሮ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እስመ ፡ ነቢያት ፣ አቅደሙ ፡ ዜንዎ ፡ ዕበዩ ፡=፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊ ት ፡ነቢይ ፡ ወሀብከ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይፈርሁከ ፣ ከመ ፣ ያምስጡ ፡ እምገጸ ፡ ቀ
45. Exaltation of the cross. - The brothers answered and said to him: "You have explained (a) wonderful (thing) and you have uttered (a) good (thing). But, from now on, it is fitting * that we prostrate ourselves before the holy cross and that we exalt (the cross), upon which Our Lord Jesus-Christ was crucified." Jacob answered and said: "Yes, (O my) brothers, it is right that we exalt it and that we honor it enormously, for the prophets began by announcing its greatness. The prophet David said: You have given a sign for those who fear you, so that they may escape from the face of

the bow. Again, he said: Do with us the sign of good. Our enemies will see it and they will be confounded. The prophet Ezekiel said: The Lord said to me: Enter into the middle of Jerusalem. Put this sign on the forehead of the men who are sad because of all * the sins, which happen among them; and he said to these (men), while I was hearing him: Enter into the city and pierce them. Let your eye not have pity on them. Do not have pity on the old man, on the adolescent, on the virgin, on the children, on the women. Kill (them) and cut (them) all off. As for those who will have this sign, do not approach them. The wise Solomon said: Blessed is the tree from (the top of) which the Just One appeared.

Isaiah knew, through the Holy Spirit, the wood that became that of the cross. He said: (The cross will come) from the wood of cedar, boxwood, myrtle, cypress and poplar so that they may believe * that (it is) the hand of the Lord (which) has done (this) and that (it is) the Holy One of Israel (which) has made this seen. The prophet Moses (said): The bitter water, he made it sweet by the wood. It is thus that Our Savior abolished the bitter counsel of the devil, and, through the wood of the cross, made it sweet in the heart of the faithful. (The same goes for) the stone

that (Moses) struck with the staff and from (which) came out twelve springs for the children of Israel. This is the sign of the side of the Lord which one pierced with a lance. As for the stone, it is Christ * regarding whom the prophets prophesied."

46. The Ascension of Christ. - The brothers answered and said to him: "Behold: we have believed and we have admitted what you have said. But, now, make us understand (this): Did Christ ascend to heaven with the flesh that he took from us or not? Indeed, it is said that Christ ascended (to heaven) with the flesh. As for us, we do not believe this." Jacob said: "Yes, (O my) brothers, he ascended (to heaven) with the flesh. Concerning this the prophets prophesied. The prophet David said: The Lord has ascended in jubilation and Our Lord at the sound of the trumpet.
ወአሞጽኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ ይቤ ፣ ወአሌ" ፡ ለሰብእ ፣ በእንተ ፣ መሲሕ ፡ ዘይገብሩ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ በጽሐ ፣ ወይትሌዐል ፡ እምድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኅያል ፡ ስ ሙ ፡ዘኩሎ ፡ ይመልክ ፡፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ተጽእነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ወሰረረ" ፡
The prophet Amos said: Woe to men because of the Messiah and what they will do when he comes. The Lord will rise from the earth into heaven, the Lord whose name is powerful (and) who dominates everything * The prophet David said: (The Lord) is carried upon the Cherubim and he flies upon the wings of the wind. Again, he said: The Lord has thundered in the heavens. The Most High is near

The Lord, the God of the strong, is this king of glory.

Again, he said: I have risen from the peoples and I have risen from the earth. The Lord of the strong is with us and the God of Jacob is our refuge * Furthermore, he said: The Lord has risen above the heavens and his glory has been in all the earth. Somewhere, he said: Indeed, your mercy is great up to the heavens and your justice up to the clouds. Again, he said: You have ascended into the high (places), having made captivity captive. You have given your grace to the sons of man. You have redeemed the souls of the chained (beings). This is what was said concerning the ascension of Christ in the flesh. Behold: the prophecy of the prophet Isaiah has been fulfilled, who said: He said to the chained (beings): Come out; and to those who are in darkness: Appear. David said: Sing to the Lord who has ascended into heaven * towards the East. Again, he said: The Lord, our God, has risen. Let one prostrate


[p. 629] {#po.3.p629}
ቤ ፡ ተለዐለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክነ ፡= ወይስግዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ መቅደ ሱ ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ተለዐለ ፡ ዘይኬንና ፡ ለምድር፡፡
before him on the mountain of his temple. Furthermore, he said: He who will judge the earth has risen.

The prophet Zechariah said: The Lord, my God, will come and all his saints will be with him. Again, he said: His feet will stand, on that day, upon the Mount of Olives which is opposite Jerusalem, towards the East. Indeed, (it is) from the Mount of Olives (that) Our Lord Jesus Christ ascended (to heaven), in the flesh. His disciples prostrated themselves before him. Hannah, the mother of Samuel, said: The Lord has ascended to heaven (and) has thundered.
ይቤ ፡ እንባቆም ፡ ነቢይ ፣ ከደነ ፡ ሰማያተ ፡ ”ሥኑ ፡፡ ወመልአ ፣ ምድረ ፡ ስብሐቲ ሁ = ወጸዳጵኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ውእቱ ፡፡ ወአቅርንት ፡ ውስተ ፣ እደዊሁ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ በጽንዐ" ፣ ኅይሉ ።፡ ናሁኬ ፡ ለበውነ ፣፡ አኀው ፣፤ ዙሎሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ፣ ይሴብሕ ዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ አስመ ፣፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ዐራቄ ፡ ወረሰየ ፣ ፍቅረ ፣ ወሰላመ ፣፡ ማእከሎ ሙ ወኢሳይያስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡፤ እትነሣእ!" ፣ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይእዜ ፡ እሴባ ሕ ፡ ወይእዜ ፡ እትሌዐል ።፡
The prophet Habakkuk said: * His beauty covers the heavens and his glory fills the earth. As for his splendor, it is like the light. Horns are in his hands. He produced love by the power of his strength. Behold then: we have understood (it), (O my) brothers: all peoples will glorify Christ, for He Himself became the reconciler and established love and peace among them. Isaiah said: Behold: I will rise, said the Lord. Now, I will be glorified and, now, I will be raised.
አውሥአ ፡ ፳ ፡ እምአኅው ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ ኢለበውክ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ቃለ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ናሁ ፡ እትነሣእ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ይመስል፣ዘኮነ፣ህልወ ፡ በመዋ
47. Eternity of the Word. - One among the brothers, called Tewoderos, answered and said to him: "As for you, you have not understood this word that the prophet said: Behold: I will rise. It resembles this (word) which became

ዕለ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነ”ቢይ ።፡ ተሰጥወ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወይቤ ፣ አእሁየ ፣ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔ ር ህልው ፡ ወይትነበብ ፡ ወትረ ፡ በአፈ ፡ ነቢያቲሁ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ዳዊት ፣ ነቢይ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ስሙ ፡=፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ለትውልደ ፡ ትውል ድ ፡=፡ ይቤ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፣፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ አምላክነ ፣ ወኢናመልክ ፡ ባዕደ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡፡
true, in the days of the prophet Isaiah." * Jacob answered and said: "O my brother, the word of the Lord is true and is stated always by the mouth of his holy prophets. As the prophet David said: Before the sun his name existed. Before the moon (it existed) for the generations of generations. The prophet Jeremiah said: This one is our God. We do not render worship to another with him.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፣ አንሶሰወ ፡ ወምስለ ፣ ሰብእ ፣ አስተርአየ ። ወ ኢዮብኒ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ይቤ ፤ ኣአምር ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይገምሮ ፡ ቤት ፣ ለዘሀለዎ ፡ ይፈ ውሰኒ ፣ ወእንዘ ፡፣ ዓዲ ፣ ሀለውኩ ፡ ዲበ፣ ምድር ።፡ ይቤ ፤ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በእን ተ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘነ”ስአ ፡ እማርያም ፣ ድንግል ። ናሁ ፡ መዋዕል ፣ ይመጽእ ፡ ይቤ ፣ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ አሐንጽ ፡ ለዳዊት ፣ መንበረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ ይነግሥ ፡፣ ወኣገብእ ፡ ርትዐ ፡ ወጽ ድቀ ፡ ይነግሥ ፣ በመዋፅሊሁ ፣ ወያድኅን ፣ ይሁዳ ፣ ወእስራኤል ፡ በፍቅር ፣ ወሰላም ፡፣ ወይሰመይ ፡ ስሙ ፡ በነቢያተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸባኦት ፡፡
48. The Incarnation. - After that, he walked on the earth and he appeared with men. The righteous Job said: I know however that a house does not contain him who must heal me, while again I will find myself on the earth. The prophet Isaiah said concerning the flesh that (Christ) took * from the Virgin Mary: Behold: days will come, said the Lord. I will build for David a throne of justice. He will reign. I will hand over (to him) righteousness and justice. He will reign in his days, and he will save Judah and Israel, in love and peace. His name will be called in the prophets of the Lord Sabaoth.
ርኢክሙኑ' ፡ አኀው ፡ ከመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ህልው ፡ ውስተ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ወ በደኃሪ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ ነሥአ ፣ እምኔነ ፤ ሥጋ ፡=፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ኢንክል ርእየ"፣መለኮቶ ፡ ክ ሠተ ። አውሥኡ ፡ አኅው ፡ ወይቤል ፡ እሙነ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነበበ ፡ በአፈ፣፡
49. The Word spoke through the prophets. - Have you seen, (O my) brothers, that the Word of the Lord was existing in the prophets and that, in the last days, he took from us the flesh. However, we cannot see his hidden divinity." The brothers answered and said to him: "You have said (a thing) true: (namely) that (the Word) himself spoke by the mouth

ነቢያት ፡ ወበእንቲአሁ ፡ "ተነበዩ ። ወለሊሁሰ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በአፈ ፣ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ።፡ ቀነዉ ኒ ፡ እደውየ ፡፤ ወእገርየ ።፡ ወጐለቁ' ፡ አእጽምትየ ። እሙንቱሰ ፤ ጠየቆሙ ፣ ወተ ዐወሩኒ ። ወተካፈሉ ፡ አልባስየ ፣ ለርእሶሙ ።፡ ወተዓፀዉ" ፣ ዲበ ፣ አራዝየ፡ ወካ ዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ዘይወስድዎ ፣ ለተጠብሖ ።= ጥዩቀ ፡፥ነበበ ፡ በአፈ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ዘገብሩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አበዊነ ፡=
of the prophets and that (it is) concerning him * (that) they have prophesied. He himself said by the mouth of the prophet David: They have nailed my hands and my feet and they have counted my bones. As for them, they have observed them and they have disdained me. They have divided among themselves my clothes, (to attribute them) to themselves, and they have cast lots upon my garment. Again, he said: (I was) like a sheep that one leads, to be slaughtered. (The Word) spoke clearly, by the mouth of the prophets, of everything that our fathers did against Him."
አውሳእክዎሙ ፡ አነ ፣ ዮሴፍ ፡ ኃጥእ ፣ ወአባሲ ፡ ወእቤ ፡ ርቱዕ ፡ ለነ ፡ አኅው ፡ ን እመን ፡ በክርስቶስ ፣ እስመ ፣ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ ዜነዉ ፡፤ በእንቲአሁ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፣ ነቢይ ፡ ይትሌአል ፣፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፣ መልዕልተ ፣ አርያም ፡ በ ጽድቅ ፡ ወክፅበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ይሬእዩ ፡ ወይትፌሥሁ ፡ እለ ፣ ይነብሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳ ሌም ፡ እስመ ፡ ተሰብሐ ፡ በማእከሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ እስራኤል
I answered them: "I, Joseph, sinner and criminal, have said: It is just for us, (O my) brothers, to believe in Christ, for the Law and the prophets have announced concerning him. As the prophet Isaiah said: The Lord will rise alone above * the high (places), in justice. Again, he said: (The) will see and will rejoice those who will dwell in Jerusalem, for the Holy One of Israel has been glorified in their midst.
ይቤ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እፎ ፡ ተነበዩ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይትሌዓል ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ዳእሙ ፣፡ በሥጋ ፡ ዘነ ስአ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ ወበውእቱ ፣ ሥጋ ፣ አስተርአየ ፣ ወአንሶዕሰወ ፡ ምስሌነ ።፡ በከመ ፡ ይ ቤ ፡ ሚክያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ በደኃሪ ፡ መዋዕል ፣ ያስተርኢ ፣ ደብረ ፣ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ድሉት ፣ ውስተ ፣ አርእስተ" ፣ አድባር ፣ ወይትሌዓል ፣ መልዕልተ ፣፤ አውግር፣፣
Jacob said: "How have they prophesied that he would rise, if not only in the flesh that he took from us? (It is) in this flesh (that) he appeared and that he walked with us. As the prophet Micah said: It will happen, in the last days, (that) the mountain of the Lord will appear splendid in the summits of the mountains and will rise above

the hills. The peoples will run towards it. Many peoples will go (towards it) and will say: Come, let us go up to * the mountain of the Lord and to the house of the God of Jacob; one will make us see his way and we will go to his house, on his trace, for (it is) from Zion (that) the Law will go out, and the Word of the Lord (will go out) from Jerusalem, that is to say the Holy Gospel of the New Testament that Christ brought. As for us, (O my) brothers, we will not see the peoples coming to (his) doctrine, but only (this will take place) from year to year. As for the faithful who will have believed in Christ, every day, they will learn the good way of the New Testament."
ርዐት" ፍ አውሥኡ ፡ አኅው ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ ትቤ"፣ ኦያዕቆ”ብ ፡ እምድኅረሰ ፡ መጽአ፣ እግዚእነ ፤ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኢመፍትው ፡ ለነ ፡ ንሀየው ፡ በሕገ ፡ አይሁድ ።፡፡ በከ መ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ነቢየ ፡ ያነሥእ ፡ ለክሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘከማየ ፡ ስምዕ ዎ ፡ ወተአዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡=፡
50. The Church. - The brothers answered and said to him: "You have spoken clearly, O Jacob. * But, after Our Lord Jesus Christ has come, it is not necessary that we live by the Law of the Jews. As the prophet Moses said: The Lord will raise up for you a prophet who (will be) like me. Listen to him and obey him.
ናሁኬ ፡ አእመርነ! ፡ ከመ ፡ ተውሀበ ፣ ለሙሴ ፡ ብሉይ ፡ ሕግ ፡=፡ ወሐዲስ ፡ ስርዓ ትሰ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፣፡ ክርስቶስ ።፡ ወባሕቱ ፣ ነሐሥሥ ፡ ትንግረነ ፡ እምቅዱሳ
Behold then: we know that the Old Law was given to Moses, but that the New Testament (was given) to Our Lord Jesus Christ. However, we ask (you) to explain to us (the doctrine, drawn) from the Holy Scriptures concerning the Holy Church, for it was explained in


[p. 633] {#po.3.p633}
ት' ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ በአንተ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እስመ ፣ ተነግረት ፡ በኦሪት ።፡ ተሰጥዎሙ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያንሰ ፣ ቀዲሙ ፡ ተነግረ ፡ በኦሪት ፡ ወበነ ቢያት ፡ ወሰበኩ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ፡=፡ ር
the Law." Jacob answered them and said (to them): "Certainly, the Church was explained formerly in the Law and in the prophets. (These) have preached concerning her.
ይቤ ፡ በኦ"ሪት ፡ ከመ ፡ አውሰበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ያልቆብ ፡ ክልኤተ!" ፡ አዋልዲሁ ፡ ለላባ ፡ ቀዳሚት ፡ ልያ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ኢኮነት ፡ ላሕየ ።፡ ወይእቲሰ ፣ ምሳሌ ፡ ዘትካት ፡ ምዙራቦ ሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፤ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ዐይነ ፣ ወኢይሬአዩ"።= ወዳግሚት ፡ ራሔል ፡ እንተ ፡ትንእስ ፡ ለሕይወት ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወፍቅርት ፣ በኅበ ፡ አዝማዲ ሃ ፡ ወመካን ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወይእቲሰ ፡ አምሳሊሃ ፡ ለሐዲስ ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ለአለ ፣ አምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡፡
(Moses) said in the Law * that our father Jacob married the two daughters of Laban. The first was Leah. However, she was not pretty. This one is the type of the ancient temple of the Jews. As the prophet Isaiah said: They have eyes, but they do not see. The second was Rachel, who was much (more) small for age and was cherished by her parents. She was barren. As for her, she is the image of the new (and) holy Church of those who believe in Christ.
በከመ ፡ ተነበየ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ፣ ወይቤ ፣ ትትፌሣሕ ፡ መካን ፡ እንተ፣ ኢትወልድ ፡ ወትሴባሕ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወትትሐሠይ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኢትፀንስ ፡ እምእንተ ፡ ባ ቲ፡ምተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብቡኅ ፣ "ውሉዳ ፡ ለማዕስብ ። ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አርሕቢ ፡ መክነ ፡ ማኅደርኪ ፡ ወአርኅዊ! ፡ አናቅጸ ፡ ቤትኪ ፣ ወአኑጊ ፣ መታክለ ፡ ደብተራኪ ፡ እስመ ፡ ታስተርእዬ፤ ፡ እምየማን ፡ ወአምጸጋም ፣ ወዘርእኪ‹ ፣ ይወርሱ ፡ አህጉረ ፣ አሕ ዛብ።
As Isaiah prophesied concerning her and (as) he said (of her): She will rejoice the barren who does not give birth. She will be glorified enormously and she will exult, (she) who will not conceive, more than she who has a husband, for numerous will be * the sons of the widow. The Lord said: Widen the place of your habitation, open the doors of your house and lengthen the stakes of your tent, for you will appear by the right and by the left, and your offspring will inherit the cities of the peoples.


[p. 634] {#po.3.p634}
ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፣ አብርሂ ፣ አብርሂ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ በጽሐ ፣ ብርሃንኪ ፡ ወስብሐተ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ሠረቀ ፡ ላዕሌኪ ። ናሁ ፣ ጽልመት ፡ ደፈና ፣ ለምድር ፡ ወቆባር ፡ ላዕ ለ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ወለኪሰ ፡ ብርሃነ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወስብሐቲሁ ፡ ወነዋ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ላ ፅሌኪ ፣ ወየሐውሩ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ በብርሃንኪ ፣ ወአሕዛብኒ ፡ በጸዳልኪ =፡ አንሥኢ ፡ አዕ ይንቲኪ ፡ ወነጽሪ ፣ አውደኪ ፡ ወርእዬ ፣ "ከመ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፣ ደቂቅኪ ፣ አውደኪ ፣ ወአምጽኡ ፣፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እምርሁቅ ፣ ውሉዲኪ ፡ ወአዋልዲኪ ፡ ይጸውርዎን ፣ በመታ
Again, he said: Shine, shine, (O) Jerusalem. Your light has arrived and the glory of the Lord has risen upon you. Behold: darkness has covered the earth and obscurity (has extended) over the peoples. But for you these are the light of the Lord and his glory; behold they have appeared upon you. Kings will go to your light and peoples to your splendor. Lift your eyes, look around you and see * that your children have gathered around you, that one has brought from afar all your sons and that one has carried your daughters on the shoulders.
ክፍት ወዳዊትኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፣ ይቤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡= ስምዒ ፣ ወለትየ ፣ ወርእ 6፤፣ ወአጽምኢ ፡ እዝንኪ ። ወርስዒ ፣ ሕዝበኪ ፣ ወቤተ ፣ አቡኪ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ እግዚእኪ ።፡ ይሰግዳ ፣ ሎቱ ፣ አዋልይ ፤ ጢሮስ ፣ በአምኃ = አዋልደ ፡ ጢሮስ ዝይ ቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ እለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ወተጠምቁ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ቤ ተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ቅድስት ፡፡ ወቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያንሰ ፣ መርዓቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተነ ግረ ፡ በመኃልየ ፡ ማኅልይ ፥ ብዙኀ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወዘከመ ፣ ይብላ ፣ "መርኣዊ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ለመርዐቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንተ ፡ ነበረት ፡ትካት ፡ እንዘ ፣ መካን ፡ይ
The prophet David said concerning the Church: Hear, (O) my daughter, see, listen (with) your ears, forget your people and the house of your father. Because (God) is your Lord, the daughters of Tyre will prostrate themselves before him with gifts. The daughters of Tyre (word) that the prophet said, these are the peoples who believe in Christ and are baptized in the one (and) holy Church. As for the Church, she is the bride of Christ, as (this) was explained in the Song of Songs, many times, and as the bridegroom, that is to say Christ, said * to his bride, (that is to say to) the Church, who remained formerly, while she was barren.
እቲ። ወይእዜሰ ፣ ይደሉ ፡ ኦአኀው ፣ ንነስሕ ፤ እምእከይነ ፡ ወንሕድግ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ አበዊ
But now, it is fitting, O (my) brothers, that we repent

ነ ፤ እስመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በብዝኅ ፡ ምሕረቱ ፡ ወበእበየ ፣ ሣህሉ ፣ ሠምረ ፣ ይሚ ጠነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፣ በእንተ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እ ለ ፤ ተጸውዑ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሐዳስ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡፡ ሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ኩልክሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ። ወይሴብሕዎ ፣ ዙሎሙ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጸንዐት' ፡ ምሕረቱ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ወጽድቁሰ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣ ይሄሉ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ትልዉ ፡ እንከ ፡ አ ኀዊ”ነ ፣ ውስተ ፣ ስብሐተ ፣ መዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወንአመን ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ልብነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡=፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በአፈ ፣ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ እንተ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አነ ፣ እሠይም ፣ ውስተ ፡፣ ጽዮን ፡ እብነ ፣ ውስተ ፣ መሰረታ ፣ እብነ ፤፣ ማዕዘንት ፣ ክብርተ ፡ ወኅሪተ ፣ እንተ ፣ ብዙኅ ፣ ሜጣ ፣ ውስተ ፣ መሠረታ፣፤ ወዘአምነ ፣፡ ባቲ ፡ ኢይትሐፈሩ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አነ ፡ እሬስዮ ፡ ለዕንቀኪ ፡ ዘያ ኀቱ ፡ ወመሰረትኪ ፣ ዘመረግድ፤" ፣ ወአረፍትኪ ፣ ዘከርከዴን" ፣ ወአናቅጽኪ ፣ ዘቢረሌ ፡ ወአልባሲኪ ፡ ዘበእንቀ፥ ፡ ክቡር ፤ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቅኪ ፡ ምሁራን ፣ እምኅበ ፣ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብዙ”ኅ ፣ ሰላም ፣ ደቂቅኪ ፡ ወናሁ ፣፡ ትትሐነጺ ፣ በጽድቅ ፣ ወረሐ ቂ" ፡ እምዐመጻ ፡ ወኢትፍርሂ ፡ ወኢይቅረብኪ ፡ ረዓድ ፡፡ ናሁ ፡ ፈላስያን ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኀቤኪ ፡ ወይትመሐፀኑ ፡ ብኪ ፣ ወታድኅንዮሙ ፡፡
of our malice and that we abandon the work of our fathers, for the Lord, in the multitude of his mercy and in the greatness of his clemency, was pleased to bring us back to his own knowledge. As the prophet David said concerning the peoples, who are called into the new (and) holy Church: Glorify the Lord, all you peoples. All generations will glorify him, for his mercy has been strong upon us. Certainly, the justice of the Lord will exist forever. Let us follow (it) then, (O) my brothers, * in the glorification of the psalm of David, and let us believe with all our heart with the peoples who have believed in Christ. 51. Christ, cornerstone. - The Lord said, by the mouth of the prophet Isaiah, concerning the Church of the peoples: Behold: I myself will place a stone in Zion, in its foundation, a corner stone, precious, chosen, of a considerable price. (I will place it) in its foundation. He who will have believed in it, will not be confounded. Again, he said: Behold: I myself will make (your trimming) in gems that will shine, your foundation in emeralds, your walls in carbuncles, your doors in beryls, your clothes in (fabrics, adorned) with precious gems. All your children will be instructed by the Lord. * Your children will be in considerable peace. Behold: you will be built in justice. Move away from iniquity, do not fear, and let trembling not approach you. Behold: strangers will come towards you and will put themselves under your guard. You will save them.


[p. 636] {#po.3.p636}
ወእመሰ ፡ ኦአኅው ፡ እለ ፣ የአምኑ ፡ በእብን ፣ ርአሰ ፣ ማእዘንት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፣ ወኢይትኅፈሩ ፡ ርቱዕኬ ፡ ንህነ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ንእመን ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፤ ኢን ትኀፈር ፡ ለዓለም ፡፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አለ ፣ አምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ =፡ ናሁ ፡ አነብ ር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ አብነ ፡ አስትቶ ፣ ወዕቅፍት ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘየአምን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ኢይፈር ህ ፡ ፍርሀት ፡፡ ወእለሰ ፣ ኢየአምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ የኅስሩ ፡ ወይ”ትኅፈሩ ፡ እስመ ፡ ው እቱ ፡ ኮኖሙ ፡ እብነ ፡ ዕቅፍት ፡=፡ ወበእንተዝ ፣ ኦአኅው ፡ ተምዕዐነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዘረወነ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ኩሉ፣ም ድር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ኅሱራነ ፡ ወምኑናነ ፡ በዝዓለም ፡ ወበዘይመጽእ ፡ ዓለም ፣ ይፈደፍድ ፣ ነኔነ ፡ አመ ፣ ይመጽእ ፣ በስብሐቲሁ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ መኩንነ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ ይኩንን ፤ ሕያዋ ነ! ፣ ወሙታነ ፡፡ አሜሃ ፣ ይትፌነዉ ፡ እለ ፤ ኢአምኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ገሃነም ፡ እ ንተ ፣፡ ኢትጠፍአ ፡ ወአልባቲ ፣ ማሀለቅተ ።፡ ወአለሰ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፣ በዝዓለም ፣ ቡሩካን ፣ አለ ፣ ይሴብሕዎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔ ር ፡ በውስተ ፣ አሐቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንተ ፣ ሐዋርያት ፣ ዘበውስቴታ ፣ መምህ ራን ፡ ከመ ፤ መሠረት ፡ እ”ለ ፥ ይሜህሩ ፣ ለሕዝብ ፡ ፍኖቶ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ኢየሱስ ፡
If, O (my) brothers, those who will believe in the stone, head of the corner, that is to say Christ, will not be confounded, it is therefore right that, we Jews, we believe in Christ, so that we are not confounded forever. The prophet said concerning those who will have believed in Christ: Behold: I will place in Zion a stone of contempt and of scandal. Every (being) who will believe in it will certainly not fear. As for those who will not believe in Christ, they will be debased and they will be confounded, * for He Himself became for them the stone of scandal. 52. The punishment of the incredulous. - That is why, O (my) brothers, the Lord became angry against us and scattered us throughout the earth. We have become confounded (beings) and rejected (beings) in this world. In the world that will come our torment will grow. When he comes in his glory, Christ, judge of justice, to judge the living and the dead. Then, those who have not believed will be sent into the fire of Gehenna, which will not be extinguished and will not have an end. Those who will believe in him in this world will be blessed (beings), who will glorify the Lord in the one (and) apostolic Church, in which the Doctors are like foundation, (they) who * teach the people the way of Our Lord Jesus Christ. Their light will rise in the whole world. As the prophet said: Behold: I myself will make (your trimming) in gems that will shine and your foundation in emeralds (Your trimming in)


[p. 637] {#po.3.p637}
ክርስቶስ ፣ ወይሠርቅ ፡ ብርሃኖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ነቢይ ፡ና ሁ ፡ አነ ፡ እሬስዮሙ ፡ ለእንቀ'ኪ ፣ ዘያሐቱ ፣ ወመሰርተኪ ፡ ዘመረግድ ፡ ፅንቀ'ኪ ፡
gems that will shine and your foundation in emeralds signify the holy Fathers of the Church."
ዘያሐቱ ፡ ወመሰረትኪ ፡ ዘመረግድ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳነ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡፡ አውሥኡ ፡ አኅው ፣ ወይቤልዖ ፡ ብጹዕ ፡ አንተ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ዘተውሀበ!' ፡ ለከ ፡ አእ ምሮ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ምስጢረ ፣ ጥዩቀ ፡ ትምሕርተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እ ስመ ፡ ትካት ፡ እንዘ ፡ አይሁዳዊ ፡ አንተ ፣ ኢኅደረ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፣ ዝንቱ ፣ ጥበብ ።፡ ወድኅ ረሰ ፣ ኅርየከ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወአብርሀ ፣ አዕይንተ" ፣ "ልብከ ፣ ወአለበወከ ፣ ታእምር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ተሰጥወ ፡ ያፅቆብ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አኅው ፡ ናሁ ፣ ቀዲሙ ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ስሕተ ተ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወእጸልእ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወዘክሮቶ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወኢይፈቅድ ፡ እ ስማፅ ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ዘተነበዩ ፣ በእንቲአሁ ። ዘእንበለ ፣ ዳእሙ ፡ ነበርኩ ፣ እን ዘ ፡ አትመነገን ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በዙሉ ፣ እኩይ ፣ ወእጹአእሎሙ ፣ ፈድፋዴ ፡=፡ ወሶ በ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ፉቃ ፡ንጉሠ ፡ ሮም ፡ በቀኦስጥንጥንያ ፡ ሀገር ፡ አጽሐብክዎሙ!" ፡ ለክርስቲያ ን ፡በዓቢይ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወሐዘን ፣፡ ወተመሰልኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አፍራሲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሳለ ቅ ፡ ወእፀርፍ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡፡ ወበመዋዕለ ፣ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፣ ነበርኩ ፡ አስተዋድ ዮሙ፣ ከመ ፣ ይቅትልዎሙ ፡ ወያውዕይዎሙ ፡ በእሳት ።፡፡ ወበአንጾኪያ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ስፍነ ፡
53. Conversion of Jacob. - The brothers answered and said to him: "You are a blessed (being), (O) Jacob, you to whom it was given to know this great mystery. Obviously, it is the doctrine of the Holy Spirit. Indeed, formerly, when you were a Jew, this wisdom did not dwell in you. But, after Christ had chosen you, he illuminated the eyes * of your intelligence and he made you understand, so that you know everything." Jacob answered and said: "(O my) brothers, behold: formerly I dwelt in the error of Satan; I hated the law of the Christians and the mention of Christ, and I did not want to hear the prophecy of the prophets, who had prophesied concerning him: but only I dwelt in plotting (artifices) against the Christians in all (sorts of) evils and I outraged them enormously. When Fouga reigned, king of Rome, in the city of Constantinople, I annoyed the Christians with great torments and sorrows, and I became like a horseman in ridiculing (them) and (in) cursing (them). In the days of Mark the Evangelist * I persevered in incriminating them, so that one would kill them and burn them by fire. In the city of Antioch, I dwelt many times, in seeking to kill the Christians and, (during) many days, I


[p. 638] {#po.3.p638}
ጊዜ ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አኀሥሥ ፣ ይቅትሎሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፤ ወብዙኀ ፡ ዕለተ ፣ ወገር ክዎሙ ፡ በአዕባን ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይመስለኒ ፤ ዘኣቀርብ ፣ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ምግባረ ፣ ሠናየ ። ወበዘ ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ግብር ፡ ነበርኩ ፡ በውርዙትየ ፡ ወዓቢይ ፡ ጽንዐ ፡ ኅይልየ ፡ መ ጠነ ፡ ፳፣ብእሲ ፡፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፣፡ ሜጠኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ አበውየ ፡ አምላ ከ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ ወበምሕረቱ ፡ አግብአኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍቅሩ ፡ ወረሰ የኒ ፡ ክርስቲያናዌ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃድየ ፡=፡ ወበብዝኀ ፡ ሣህሉ ፡ አ”ጽንዐኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አ ሚን ፡ ወወደየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ አንብቦ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ዐቂበ' ፡ ቃላ ተ ፡ ዘውስቴቶሙ ። ወእምዝ ፣ ለበውኩ ፡ ከመ ፣ መጽአ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ በመዋዕለ ፣ አው ግስጦስ ፡ ቄሳር ፣ ንጉሠ ፣ ሮም ፣ ወተወልደ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ልሄም ፡ ዘይሁዳ ፡ እማርያም ፡
stoned them while it seemed to me that I was offering to the Lord a good work. (It is) in such a work (that) I dwelt in my youth. The strength of my valor was as great as (that of) twenty men. After that, the Lord, the God of my fathers, the God of Abraham, of Isaac and of Jacob, converted me and, in his mercy, made me return into his love. He made me a Christian despite my will and, in his considerable clemency, strengthened me * in the faith. He put in my heart the love of the reading of the Holy Scriptures and of the observation of the words that were in them. Then, I understood that Christ had come in the days of Augustus Caesar, king of Rome, and was born in Bethlehem of Judah, of Mary, of the Holy Virgin, daughter of Joachim. Obviously, that is without error."
እምቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፣ ወለተ ፣ ኢያቄም ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ አስሕቶ ፡፡ አውሥአ ፡፳፡፤እምአኀው ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስኩ ፡ አርእየነ ፡ እመ ፣ ትክል ፡ አይድዖተነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ይእቲ =፡ ተሰጥዎ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ጥዩቀ ፡ ሕዝበ ፣ አይሁድ ፡ ጸላእያኒሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ድን”ዙዛነ ፣ አልባ ብ ፡ ኢይትመየጡ ፡ ወኢየአምኑ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመጽእ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ።፡ ሕያው ፡ እ ግዚአብሔር ፡ እመ ፡ ሰምዑከ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ከመ ፡ ትቤ ፣ ዘንተ ፡ ያውእዩከ ፣ በእ ሳት።
54. Genealogy of Mary. - One among the brothers, called Isaac, answered and said to him: "By grace, make us see, if you can explain (it) to us, that Mary is of the house of Judah." Jacob answered and said: "Obviously, the people of the Jews have been the enemy of the Lord. * Indolent of heart, they will not convert and they will not believe, until the punishment comes upon them. The Lord is living. If the Christians hear you say this, they will burn you by fire."


[p. 639] {#po.3.p639}
ይቤሎ ፡ እሬእየከ' ፡ ከመ ፡ ታስተዋድየኒ ፡ በከመ ፣ ልማድከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘነበርከ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ታቀትሎሙ ፡ አምነ ፡ያዕቆብ ፣ ኀጢአቶ ፡ ወይቤ ፣ እሙነ ፡ ገበርኩ ፡ ዙሎ ፡ እኩየ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይመስለኒ ፣ ዘኣሜኒ ፤ ከመዝ ፣ ዘመስዋዕተ ፣ እገብ ር ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ።፡ ወይእዜኒ" ፡ እስእሎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ይስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ኀጢአትየ ፡ ወአበሳየ ።፡ አውሥኡ ፡ አኅው ፣ ወይቤልዖ ፡ ለይስሐቅ ፡ አር"ምም ፡ዘን ተ ፡ነገረ ፡ እስከ ፡ ያጤይቀነ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወለተ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ይእቲ ፡፡ ይቤ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ያአምር" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ኦአኀኅው ፣ እመ ፡ አለበወኒ ፣ ከመ ፡ ማ ርያም ፤ ወለተ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኢእገብእ ፣ ዳግመ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ዘእንበ ለ ፡ ዳአሙ ፡ እከውን ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ።፡፡ እስመ ፡ አንሰ ፣ ኀሠሥኩ ፣ ብዙኀ ፣ መጻሕፍተ ፤ ወኢረከብኩ ፡ ዘያየድዐኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እምዘመይ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ ወን ጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጽሁፍ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ እምአብር፣ሃም ፡ እስከ ፣ ዮሴፍ ፣ ዘፈሐራ ፡ ለማርያ ም።፤ድንግል።
(Isaac) said to him: "I see you incriminating me, according to your habit (of doing) with the Christians, you who have persevered in making them killed." Jacob confessed his sin and said: "Truly, I have done all (sorts of) evils upon them, while it seemed to me that I was acting well thus, (and) that I was making a sacrifice to the Lord. But now, I ask the Lord to forgive me all my sins and my faults." The brothers answered and said to Isaac: "Be silent, * until (Jacob) has demonstrated to us this thing, namely: if Mary is daughter of David." Isaac said: "The Lord knows (it), O (my) brothers. If (Jacob) makes me understand that Mary is daughter of David, I will not return, again, to the Law of the Jews, but on the contrary, I will become a Christian. Indeed, for me, I have searched many Scriptures, and I have not found (one) that has demonstrated to me that (Mary) is of the lineage of Judah, except however the Holy Gospel in (which) it is written: From Abraham until Joseph who married the Virgin Mary"
ተሰጥዉ ፡ አኀው ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ኢትሕዘን ፡ ኦአተነ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ በእንተ ፣ ዘተስእል ናክ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሐዲስ ሥርዐት ፣ ውእቱ ፡፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ መ”ፍትው ፣ ትክሥት ለነ ፣ ወትነግረነ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ እፎ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ማርያም ፡ እምዘርአ ፣ ዳዊት ፡፡ ወእመሰ ፡ ኢአ
The brothers answered and said to him: "Do not be grieved, O our brother Jacob, that we have asked you (about) this, for it is (in) the New Testament. But now, it is necessary * that you reveal to us and explain to us how Mary is of the lineage of David. Now,"


[p. 640] {#po.3.p640}
ለበውከነ' ፡ ዘንተ ፤ ምስጢረ ፣ ናሰትቶ ፣ ለክርስቶስ ። ይቤ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ኢታስትት ፡ኦ ይስሐቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ወልዱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፣ አብርሃም ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፤ በወ ንጌል ፤ ቅዱስ ፡=፡ ይቤ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፣ ኢኣሰትት ፡ አንሰ ፣ ከመ ፣ ዮሴፍ ፣ እምዘመደ"፣ ይሁዳ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘፈኅራ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወጥዩቀ ፡፣ ዘይቤ ፡ ወንጌል ፡፡ ናሁ ፡ ብዙኀ ፣ መጻሕፍተ ፡ አንበብኩ ፣ ወመጻሕፍተ ፡ ነገሥት ፣ አጳንገልኩ ፤ ወጠየቁ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ዮሌፍ ፣ እምዘርአ ፣ ዳዊት ፡ ወእምዘመደ ፣ ይሁዳ ፣፤ ውእቱ ።፡ ወበእንተ ፣፡ ማርያ ምሰ ፡ ”ኢለበውኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ እምዘርአ!" ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ይእቲ ፡፡ ይቤ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ በእንተዝሰ ፡ ርቱዕ ፣ ለከ ፣ ትሕትት ፡፣ በእንተ ፣ ልደታ ፡፣ ለማርያ ም ፡ ቡርክት፣ እስመ ፣ በእንቲአሃ ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ አስተቱ" ፡፡ ወአነኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ።፡ ወ ባሕቱ ፣ አምላከ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ኅቤነ ፡ ብእሴ ፣ ጠቢበ ፡ ወ መምህረ ፡ ወተስእልናሁ ፡፡ አውሥአ ፣ ወይቤለነ ፡ ማርያምሰ ፣ ዘያአብይዋ ፡ ክርስቲያ ን ፡ እምዘመደ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወእምዘርአ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ይእቲ ፡፡ ወዝንቱሰ ፣ መምህር ፣ አይሁ ዳዊ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ዘእምብሔረ ፡ ጥብርያዶስ ።
if you do not make us understand this mystery, we will despise Christ." Jacob said: "Do not forget, Isaac, that Joseph is a son of our father Abraham, as (it is) said (in Scripture) in the Holy Gospel." Isaac said: "As for me, I do not forget that Joseph is of the lineage of Judah, he who married the Virgin Mary. This is obviously what the Gospel says. Behold: I have read many Scriptures; I have interpreted the Scriptures of the Kings and I have noticed that Joseph is of the lineage of David and of the line of Judah. But, concerning Mary, * I have not understood that she was of the lineage of David." Jacob said: "But that is why it is right that you search concerning the generation of Mary, the Blessed one, for it is because of her that many have despised (Christ). As for me, I was like them. But the God of Abraham, of Isaac, and of Jacob sent to us a wise man and doctor. We questioned him. He answered and said to us: "As for Mary, whom the Christians exalt, she is of the line of Judah and of the lineage of David." This doctor is a Jew who is from the land of Tiberias."
ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤለነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማርያም ፣ እምዘርአ ፣ ዳዊት ፣ ወለተ ፡ ኢያቄም ፣ ወልደ ፤፡ ቅስራ ፡ ወልደ ፣ ፊልቅያ ፡ ”ወቅስራሰ ፣ እጉሁ ፡ ለማጣት ፣ ወልደ ፡ ሌዊ ፡ ዘእምወል
Again, he told us that Mary is of the lineage of David, (being) daughter of Joachim, son of Qesra, son of Filgeya *. As for Qesra, he is


[p. 641] {#po.3.p641}
ደ ፡ናታን ፡ ወልደ ፤ ዳዊት ፣፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ዘእምዘመደ ፡፤ ይሁዳ ።፡፡ ወሶበ ፣፡ ሞተ ፡ ናታን፣ አቡሁ ፡ ለያዕቆብ ፣ አውሰበ ፣ ብእሲቶ ፣ ማጣት ፡ ወልደ ፣ ሌዊ ፣ ወልደ ፣ መልኪ፣ ወባቲ ፤ ወልደ ፡ ዘስሙ ፣ ያፅቆብ' ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ምስለ ፣ እሙ ፣ ቤተ ፣ ማጣት ፡-እጉሁ ፡ ለቅስራ ፣ አቡሁ ፣ለኢያቄም ።፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ወለደ ፣ ማጣት ፣ እምእሙ ፡ ለያፅቆብ፣ወል ደ ፤ ወሰመይዎ ፡ ኤሊ ፡ ወያዕቆብሰ ፣ ወኤሊ ፣ ፪አኀው ፡ ወአሐቲ ፣ እሞሙ ፡፣ ወአበ ዊሆሙሰ ፡ ፍሉጣን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ኤሊ ፤ አውሰበ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ብእሷቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያቅም ፣ ዘርአ ፣ ለእጉሁ ፤ ወወለደ ፣፡ ወልደ ፡ እምብእሲተ' ፡ ኤሊ ፡ እጉሁ ፥፡
brother of Matat, son of Levi, who (is descended) from the son of Nathan, son of King David, who is of the line of Judah. When Nathan, father of Jacob, died, Matat, son of Levi, son of Malki, married his wife. She had a son, called Jacob. He lived with his mother in the house of Matat, brother of Qesra, father of Joachim. Then, Matat begot (from) the mother of Jacob (another) son. He was called Eli. As for Jacob and Eli, they were two brothers. Their mother was the same. But their fathers were different. When Eli died, Jacob married his wife, in order to provide offspring for his brother. || begot a son from the wife of Eli, his brother, and he called him Joseph.
ወሰመዮ ፣፤ዮሴፍ። ዮሴ"ፍሰ ፣ ወልደ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኤሊ ፡ በሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡፡ ወኢያቄም ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቅስራ ፡ እሁ ፡ ለማጣት ፡ ደቂቀ ፣ ሌዊ ፡ እሙንቱ ፣ በዘመድ ፡ ዝውእቱኬ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ለማርያም ፣ ድንግል ፡ ናሁ ፡ ዐረዩ ፡ ዘመድ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወለተ ፡ ኢያቄ ም፣ ወልደ ፡ቅስራ ፣ እጉሁ ፡ ለማጣት ፡ ወልደ ፡ሌዊ"' ፡ ወልደ ፣ መልኪ ፡፡ ወዮሴፍሰ ፡ ወልይ ፤ አእተቱሁ ፡ ለአቡሃ ፣ ዘውእቱ ፣ ወልደ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ናታን ፡ በሥጋ ፡ወበ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፣ ወልደ ፣ ኤሊ ፣ ወልደ ፣ ማጣት ፣፤ ውእቱ ፡=፡ ወበእንተዝ" ፡ ተውሕበት ፣ ማርያም ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፣ በቤተ ፣ መቅደስ ። ኢይመስሎሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፣ ከመ ፡ ማርያ
As for Joseph, he is * son of Jacob, by the flesh, (but) son of Eli, by the rule of the Law. Joachim is son of Qesra, brother of Matat. These are children of Levi by lineage. (Joachim is therefore the father of the Virgin Mary. Behold: the lineages are equal. Mary is the daughter of Joachim, son of Qesra, brother of Matat, son of Levi, son of Malki. As for Joseph, he is the son of the brother of the father (of Mary), that is to say son of Jacob, son of Nathan, by the flesh, but, by the rule of the Law, he is son of Eli, son of Matat. This is why Mary was given to Joseph, in the house of the temple. It does not seem to the Christians that Mary (came) from heaven, but only that she is begotten thus


[p. 642] {#po.3.p642}
ም ፣ እምሰማይ ፡ ይእቲ ።፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዳእሙ ፣ ከመዝ ፡ ተወልደት ፣ እምዘርአ ፡ ዳዊት ፣ ወእምዘመደ ፣ ”ይሁዳ ፡= ወሰሚአነ ፣ ዘንተ ፣ በኀቤሁ ፣ ተፈሣሕነ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፣ በእን ተ ፡ ዘአጠየቀነ ፡ ልደታ ፡ ለማርያም ።፡ ወካዕበ ፣ ወንጌል ፡ ይነግር ፣ ከመ ፣ ኤልሳቤጥ፣ ዘመዳ ፡ ለማርያም ፡፡
from the lineage of David and the line * of Judah." Having heard this from him, we rejoiced greatly that he had demonstrated to us the generation of Mary. Furthermore, the Gospel explains that Elizabeth is the relative of Mary.
አውሥአ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ኦእሁየ ፡ ያፅቆብ ፣ እፈቅድ ፡ እሰአልከ ፣፡ አሐተ፣ ቃለ ።፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ እፈርህ ፡ ኢትመጥወኒ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፣ ከመ ፣ ኢያማስኑ ፣ ሕይወት የ ፡ እምድር ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ መኅለ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ በአምላከ ፣ አብርሃም ፡፣ ይስሐቅ ፡፣ ወያዕቆ ብ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ እምይእዜሰ፤' ፡ ኢይገብር ፡ ላዕሌከ ፣ እኩየ ፡ ወኢለመኑሂ ፡ ነዓ" ፣ ንትክየ ድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢናስምእ ፡ ነገረነ ፤ ወኢለ8 ፡ እምክርስቲያን ፡=፡ ዳአሙ ፣ ይኩን ፡ ዕቁበ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ማዕከሌነ ፡፡ ውእተ ፣፡ አሚረ ፣ ተማሐሉ ፣ ወተፋለጡ ፤ እምሀየ ፣ ወሖ ሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡፡
55. The meeting must be kept secret. - Isaac answered and said: "O my brother Jacob, I want to ask you (about) one single word. However, I fear that you might deliver me to the Christians, (and) that (they) might destroy my life from the earth." Then, Jacob swore by the God of Abraham, of Isaac, and of Jacob and said: "From now on, therefore, I will not do evil toward you, nor toward anyone. Come, let us make a pact that we will not let any of the Christians hear our exposition, but that this exposition will be kept between us." That day, * they swore among themselves. They separated from one another, (moving away) from that (place), and they went to their houses.
ይቤ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ መሐልኩ ፡ ኢአጽሕፍ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፣ ወባሕቱ ፡ በእንተ ፣ አሚኖ ትየ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ይሰሪ ፣ ኀጢአተ ፣፡ ጸሐፍኩ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘተባሐሉ ፡ በበ ይናቲሆሙ ወእምዝ ፡፣ አንበርክዎ ፣ ለወልድየ ፣ ስምዖን ፣ ድኅረ ፣ ፕኅት ፡ ያፅምእ፣ ኩሎ ፡ነገሮሙ ፡ ወይጽሐፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክርታስ ፡፡ ወአንሰ ፡፣ወትረ ፡እወፅእ ፣ አምኅቤሆ ሙ ፣ ወእኤዝዞ' ፡ ለወልድየ ፡ ይዕቀብ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘተናገሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወ
Joseph said: "I had sworn not to write this exposition. However, because of my trust that Christ will forgive (this) sin, I have written everything that they said to each other. Then, I placed my son Simeon behind the door, so that he might listen to all their exposition and write it in a volume. But I myself was always going out from them. I ordered my son to keep all the words (in) which they were conversing among


[p. 643] {#po.3.p643}
አብሎ ፡፤ ኢትርሳእ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ' ፡=፡ ወይስሐቅሰ ፡ ያነክር ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አበ ውእ ፡ ወእወፅእ ፡ ”ዙሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ስፍነ ፣ ጊዜ ፡ ትበውእ ፡ ወትወፅእ ።፡ አው ሣእክዎ ፣ ወእቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ያአምረኒ ፣ ከርሥየ ፣ ወባሕቱ ፡ ኢከሠትኩ ፣ ሎሙ ፡ ምስ ጢረ ፤ነገርየ ፡ እስከ ፡ አጽሐፍኩ፤ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ማእከሎሙ ፡5፡
themselves and I said to him: "Do not forget anything." As for Isaac, he was astonished when he saw (me) entering and leaving * all the time. He said to me: "It is many times that you enter and leave." I answered him and said to him: "Behold: (the state of) my bowels explains that." However, I did not reveal (to the Christians) the mystery of my exposition, until I had caused to be written everything that took place between them."
ስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወላዕሌነ ፡ ይኩን ፣ ምሕረቱ ፡ እምይእዜ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ለ ዓለመ ፡፤ ዓለም ፡ አሜን =
Glory to the Lord! May his mercy come upon us, from now and unto the ages of ages! Amen.
